Conclusion: 计算维度是相对于泛函而言的，不能用激活方差代理；任务加权图提供了一种良定义且可操作的降维与蒸馏方法，在低维预算下能更好保持模型预测。

Abstract: How many dimensions does a language model's computation actually use? The question is ill-posed until one names a functional. Task-weighted charts make it well-posed: low-dimensional coordinate systems fit against a chosen functional of the representation, under the functional's own metric, turning distillation into plain least squares. Across six models from three families, spanning 70m to 7B parameters, next-token prediction needs 70--90% of the residual stream's width to stay within 5% of intact perplexity, a width consumed by the rare tail of language, and the variance profile predicts none of it: two directions carry 90% of GPT-2's activation variance and almost none of its function. Dimension is per-functional: the model's own uncertainty reads from six coordinates where the full predictive distribution needs hundreds; and it grows with depth. The dissociation is exploitable: when only a few dimensions can be kept, charts trained under the functional's metric preserve the model's predictions better than variance-based or optimal linear compression.

</details>


### [281] [Lost in Perception: Isolating Perceptual and Reasoning Failures in Multimodal Physics and Geometry Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18991)
*Raj Jaiswal,Sree Krishna Uppalapati,Dhruvkumar Patel,Ria Khatoniar,Tanuja Ganu,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出五任务诊断实验，将多模态大模型在科学推理中的失败分解为感知失败、推理失败或两者兼有，发现感知错误会拖累原本能靠文本解答的问题，且失败原因随领域不同（物理→计算错误，几何→概念误用）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在科学推理基准上表现强劲，但多数研究将感知与推理视为单一可测量过程，无法区分模型究竟是"看不懂图"还是"想不明白"，因此需要一种能隔离失败来源的诊断方法。

Method: 在物理与几何基准上设计五个任务的诊断实验，通过对比原始图像、人工撰写图注以及修正图注等不同条件，分离感知与推理两类失败来源。

Result: 错误的图表解读即便在模型仅凭文本就能正确解答的问题上也会降低表现；从原始图像到人工图注准确率普遍上升；部分模型在修正图注下恢复率很高，说明其失败源于感知受阻而非推理瓶颈；失败的下游表现与领域相关，物理表现为计算错误，几何表现为概念误用。作为核心实验之外的讨论，InternS1-mini 尽管经过大量科学预训练并具备思考能力，却在每个任务上低于实验中最弱的模型，其推理轨迹经常在完成前被截断。

Conclusion: 感知与推理应被分开评估；感知失败会掩盖真实推理能力，且其后续错误类型依领域而异。此外，强预训练与思考能力并不保证稳健的科学推理表现。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs report strong performance on scientific reasoning benchmarks, yet most treat perception and reasoning as a single measurable process. We introduce a five-task diagnostic experiment across physics and geometry benchmarks that isolates failures to perception, reasoning, or both. Incorrect diagram interpretation degrades performance even on problems models solve correctly from text alone, and accuracy generally rises from raw images to human-authored captions. Recovery under corrected captions is high for some models, separating perception-blocked failures from genuine reasoning bottlenecks. Which reasoning error follows a perception failure depends on domain: physics failures resolve into calculation errors, geometry into conceptual misapplication. As a discussion beyond our core experiments, InternS1-mini, despite heavy scientific pretraining and thinking capabilities, falls below the weakest model from experiments on every task, with reasoning traces frequently truncating before completion.

</details>


### [282] [Compiled Agency: Frontier General-Purpose Coding Agents Build Winning Game Players from Bare Interaction - from Flappy Bird to StarCraft II and Civilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18996)
*Joey Xiao,Haonan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Gauntlet框架，让通用编码智能体在单次自主会话中从零编写游戏控制器，冻结后在无模型调用条件下评估，发现最新代模型在程序化roguelike、星际争霸II和Freeciv上均能独立取胜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往LLM智能体即使被研究者围绕模型构建感知、记忆、技能库、规划器或可执行策略脚手架，仍难以把游戏知识转化为胜任的对局表现。编码智能体的快速进展引出两个关键问题：前沿模型现在能否真正赢得游戏，以及能否在无人协助下自行构建完整玩家。

Method: 提出Gauntlet的"开发-冻结-评估"框架，将从小型街机到完整商业规模游戏统一接入一个刻意精简的契约：通用编码智能体只获得游戏描述、原始观测/动作接口和一个空的策略文件，没有策略、算法或架构提示。智能体在单次自主会话中与实时游戏交互实验，自行工程化出一个独立控制器；随后冻结该产物，在留出实例上评分，对局期间零模型调用。

Result: 在一个未公开的程序化roguelike上，留出集成功率跨度0-86%，并显现出明显的代际阈值：最新代系统的每一次观测会话都优于上一代的最佳会话。在完整游戏规模上，一个编译后的原始API控制器击败了星海争霸II所有公平内置AI及两种作弊变体；单会话程序在留出种子上以完全征服方式赢得Freeciv完整对局。虽然对新手AI胜率不高，但这是首次有语言智能体系统在本类游戏中独立获胜，且不依赖逐回合模型调用和手工战术层。

Conclusion: 前沿编码智能体开始具备长时程策略追踪能力，冻结后的程序可被审查。作者将这一能力称为"编译式能动性"：把开发经验编译为一个持久可执行的智能体，其架构由模型自行构建。

Abstract: LLM agents have repeatedly struggled to convert knowledge of a game into competent play, even when researchers build the agent around the model - supplying perception, memory, skill libraries, planners, or executable-policy scaffolds. Rapid progress in coding agents raises two sharper questions: can frontier models now win games at all, and can they win them unaided, building the entire player themselves? We introduce Gauntlet, a develop-freeze-evaluate framework that ports games from small arcades to full commercial-scale titles, behind one deliberately bare contract: a general-purpose coding agent receives a game description, a raw observation/action interface, and an empty policy file - no strategy, no algorithm, no architecture. In a single autonomous session the agent experiments with the live game and engineers a standalone controller; we freeze the result and score it on held-out instances with zero model calls during play. On an unpublished procedural roguelike, held-out success spans 0-86 percent and exposes a sharp generational threshold: every observed session of a newest-generation system outperforms the best session of its predecessor. At full-game scale, a compiled raw-API controller defeats every fair StarCraft II built-in AI and two cheating variants, and single-session programs win complete games of Civilization (Freeciv) by total conquest on held-out seeds. Though at modest rates against novice AI, this is a first: no prior language-agent system had won full games of this genre standalone, without per-turn model calls and a hand-crafted tactical layer. Frontier coding agents begin to track long-horizon strategy. The frozen programs are inspectable. We call this capability compiled agency: development experience compiled into a persistent executable agent whose architecture is built by the model.

</details>


### [283] [MUSE: Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on Multi-Modal Understanding in Situated Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19088)
*Luyao Zhu,Xun Wei Yee,Wei Li,Mun Thye Mak,Wee Siong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MUSE基准，用于评估大型视觉语言模型在情境化教育应用中的艺术图像理解能力，涵盖12项任务，聚焦新加坡及东南亚多元文化语境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在多模态理解上进展显著，但在教育场景中的能力评估不足。AI辅助语言学习需要模型解读艺术图像的语义、情感与文化内容并推理视觉语境，而现有基准多聚焦真实图像或特定领域教育推理，对艺术教育内容覆盖有限。

Method: MUSE将图像标注与问题生成解耦，以便生成难度可控的多样化任务并降低标注成本。基准包含12项任务，覆盖视觉感知、语义与情感解读、文化理解及组合推理，并精心挑选艺术作品，兼顾新加坡与东南亚多元文化语境及西方艺术传统，涵盖多主题与多难度层级。

Result: 对开源与专有模型的评估显示，各能力维度间存在显著差距，尤其在情感解读与组合推理方面表现突出；分析还识别出常见失败模式与关键挑战。

Conclusion: MUSE有望成为推动情境化教育应用中多模态理解研究的标准化基准，并为构建可信多模态教育模型提供参考。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable progress in multi-modal understanding, yet their capabilities in educational settings remain insufficiently evaluated. In AI-assisted language learning, models must interpret artistic imagery, understand its semantic, affective, and cultural content, and reason about visual context to support meaningful interaction. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on real-world images or domain-specific educational reasoning, providing limited coverage of artistic educational content. To address this gap, we introduce MUSE, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models on artistic image understanding in situated educational applications. MUSE decouples image annotation from question generation, enabling diverse tasks with controllable difficulty while reducing annotation effort. It comprises twelve tasks spanning visual perception, semantic and affective interpretation, culture understanding, and compositional reasoning, together with diverse artistic images deliberately curated to center Singaporean and Southeast Asian multicultural contexts alongside Western art traditions, covering multiple themes and difficulty levels. Evaluation of open-source and proprietary models reveals substantial disparities across capability dimensions, particularly in affective interpretation and compositional reasoning. Our analysis further identifies common failure modes and key challenges for developing trustworthy multi-modal models for education. We hope MUSE will serve as a standardized benchmark for advancing multi-modal understanding in situated educational applications.

</details>


### [284] [Flag Game: A Toy Model for Mechanistic Swarm Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19124)
*Elizabeth Pavlova,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了"旗帜游戏"(Flag Game)作为研究集体信念形成机制的玩具模型,揭示了集体信念坍塌与极化的现象,并发展了社会回路归因与统计力学理论两种机制解析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体的涌现协调行为开始带来严重的安全风险,其关键驱动因素是信念的快速形成与传播。为了实现集体对齐,需要对集体信念形成机制有机理性的理解,但目前缺乏相应的研究工具。

Method: 作者设计Flag Game玩具模型:隐藏的国家旗帜定义真值,每个有限理性智能体仅观察私有裁剪图像,但可交换信念并权衡同伴的社会证据。此外提出两种互补方法:(1)社会回路归因(social circuit attribution),用于预测哪个智能体及其观点对集体动态最关键,并通过智能体因果干预验证;(2)针对更大群体发展的统计力学理论。

Result: 尽管模型简单,Flag Game重现了丰富的集体现象:性能随群体规模呈非单调变化、社会意识提示与团队多样性带来准确性提升、组织结构有强烈影响。特别地,小群体下的集体信念坍塌会随群体增大转变为集体信念极化,极化导致大群体性能下降但创造了信念多样性。因果干预效力随群体增大而下降,统计力学理论与经验相图相吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作朝着"机制性群体可解释性"迈出第一步,即研究个体智能体属性及其通信如何产生涌现集体行为的科学。

Abstract: Emergent coordinated behaviors of AI agents are starting to present critical safety risks. A key phenomenon driving these behaviors is the rapid formation and spread of beliefs about the world, and mechanistic understanding is crucial for collective alignment. To this end, we introduce the Flag Game, a toy model for studying the mechanisms of collective belief formation. Concretely, a hidden country flag defines the ground truth, and each bounded agent directly observes only a private crop but can exchange beliefs and weigh social evidence from peers. Despite its simplicity, the Flag Game reproduces rich collective phenomenology: non-monotonic scaling of performance with population size, accuracy gains from social-awareness prompting and team diversity, and strong effects of organizational structure. In particular, we identify that collective belief collapse at small population sizes turns into collective belief polarization as the population grows. This polarization causes the performance decline at large population sizes, but creates diversity in collective beliefs. Finally, we dissect the mechanisms underlying collective belief collapse and polarization with two complementary approaches. We first introduce social circuit attribution, a technique to predict which agent, and what view, matters most to collective dynamics, and verify its predictions by causal interventions on agents, tracing how agent patching changes collective outcomes. However, the efficacy of causal interventions on agents decreases as the population grows. We therefore develop a statistical mechanical theory for larger populations and verify that it matches the empirical phase diagram. Together, these results take a first step toward mechanistic swarm interpretability, a science of how the properties of individual agents and their communication give rise to emergent collective behavior.

</details>


### [285] [Cognitive Extensions for Dual-Process Language Agents: Memory and Self-Reflection in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19128)
*João Meneses dos Santos,Arlindo L. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在 SwiftSage 双过程智能体上加入自适应记忆模块（AMM）与自我反思模块（SRM），在 ScienceWorld 上通过消融实验验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在交互环境中仍然脆弱：需要长时程状态跟踪、有效动作执行以及失败步骤后的恢复能力，这些环节构成了性能瓶颈。

Method: 以 SwiftSage（快速动作提议器 + 较慢规划器的双过程智能体）为基座，扩展两个模块化认知组件：AMM 用于显著性门控的情节存储与触发式检索，SRM 用于有界的执行时验证与纠正性干预；两者以特性开关形式叠加在同一执行基底上，从而在 ScienceWorld 上实现可控消融（基线、基线+AMM、基线+SRM、完整系统四种配置）。

Result: 完整系统取得最佳平均最终得分 64.62、成功率 43.17%、成功步骤效率 19.33 步；SRM 是单独贡献最强的模块。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，执行时控制是主要瓶颈；当运行时循环被稳定后，情节记忆的作用才会更充分地显现。

Abstract: Language agents remain brittle in interactive environments, where success requires long-horizon state tracking, valid action execution, and recovery from failed steps. We extend SwiftSage, a dual-process agent that combines a fast action proposer with a slower planner, using two modular cognitive extensions: an Adaptive Memory Module (AMM) for salience-gated episodic storage and trigger-driven retrieval, and a Self-Reflection Module (SRM) for bounded execution-time validation and corrective intervention. Both modules are implemented as feature-flagged extensions over the same execution substrate, enabling controlled ablations on ScienceWorld. Across four configurations---baseline, baseline+AMM, baseline+SRM, and the full system---the full system achieves the best mean final score (64.62), success rate (43.17%), and successful-step efficiency (19.33 steps), while SRM is the strongest standalone contributor. The results suggest that execution-time control is the dominant bottleneck in this setting, while episodic memory becomes most useful once the runtime loop is stabilized.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [Human-Centric Grasp State Assessment: Toward Transferring Subjective Evaluation to Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17540)
*Ryohei Kobayashi,Kosei Isomoto,Yuga Yano,Yuichiro Tanaka,Hakaru Tamukoh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个将人类隐性主观标准迁移到机器人系统的框架，实现可变形物体的恰当抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类对抓取"恰当性"的定性期望与机器人定量测量之间存在语义鸿沟；传统深度学习方法需要大量人工标注数据，且每个新物体都要重新标注，成本过高。

Method: 框架包含两部分：(1) 基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的半自动监督信号生成器，以少量人工标注试验作为上下文锚点，将主观标准传播到未标注数据；(2) 轻量级抓取状态预测器，通过时序触觉与抓取力测量序贯估计抓取状态，实现在线快速自适应。

Result: 在三种代表性可变形物体上开展实验，并进行25名参与者的人类评估研究，验证了该框架能够依据人类感知的抓取恰当性来调整抓取力。

Conclusion: 所提框架在评估任务场景下具备可行性，可在减少人工标注依赖的同时实现符合人类主观标准的可变形物体抓取力调节。

Abstract: We propose a framework that transfers tacit human subjective criteria to robotic systems for the appropriate grasping of deformable objects. Achieving such behavior is challenging because a semantic gap exists between qualitative human expectations and quantitative robotic measurements. Conventional deep learning approaches for bridging this gap also require prohibitive amounts of manually annotated data for each newly encountered object. To address these challenges, our framework integrates a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based semi-automated supervisor generator with a lightweight grasp state predictor, using a minimal set of human-annotated trials as contextual anchors to propagate subjective criteria to unannotated data. The prediction model then enables rapid online adaptation by sequentially estimating the grasp state from time-series tactile and grasping force measurements. Through experiments on three representative deformable objects and a human evaluation study with 25 participants, we demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed framework for adjusting grasping force according to human-perceived grasp appropriateness in the evaluated task setting.

</details>


### [287] [Real-Time Service Robot Replanning via Simple Button Interaction for Improved Task Success and User Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17541)
*Ryo Terashima,Yuga Yano,Koshun Arimura,Hakaru Tamukoh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实时反馈功能，让用户通过平板单键向服务机器人发送反馈，使机器人能察觉失败并重新规划动作，从而提升任务成功率与用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在真实环境中会遇到意外指令，且无法自行检测任务中的所有失败与异常情况，因此需要借助人类反馈来修正行为。

Method: 设计实时反馈系统：当机器人行为不正确时，用户可通过平板上的单个按钮直观地发送反馈；机器人据此反思失败并重新规划合适动作。通过有/无反馈功能的对比实验进行验证，问卷采用五点李克特量表评估。

Result: 加入反馈功能后，“是否觉得机器人行为出乎意料”的得分提高0.5分，“是否对机器人行为感到焦虑”的得分提高0.9分；结果支持“简单交互不会带来负面用户体验”的假设。

Conclusion: 引入该实时反馈功能可同时提升任务成功率与用户体验。

Abstract: Service robots must respond to unexpected instructions in real-world environments. However, robots cannot detect all failures and exceptions during a task. To address these issues, we propose a real-time feedback function that enables robots to modify their behavior based on human feedback. In this system, users can intuitively send feedback to the robot by pressing a single button on a tablet when the robot fails to act correctly. Robots use this feedback to consider their failures and replan appropriate actions to complete the task. We conducted experiments with and without the feedback function to verify the following hypothesis: "Simple interactions do not cause a negative user experience." All questionnaire responses are evaluated on a five-point Likert scale. After adding the feedback function, the response score for the question "Did you feel that the robot's behavior was unexpected?" improved by 0.5 points, and that for "Did you feel anxious about the robot's behavior at times?" improved by 0.9 points. These results support the study's hypothesis and indicate that incorporating this real-time feedback function can simultaneously improve task success and the user experience.

</details>


### [288] [Predictive Varanus: Combining CSP Conformance Monitoring with Predictive LTL Runtime Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17625)
*Angelo Ferrando,Matt Luckcuck,Pedro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PREDICTIVE VARANUS，将基于 CSP 的运行时验证器 VARANUS 与 LTL 预测性运行时验证结合为两阶段流水线，在机器人巡视案例中实现比标准运行时监控更早的判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时验证适合自主与机器人系统，因为它检查执行中实际观察到的行为；但其主要局限是反应式：监控器通常在系统已发生坏事件之后才发现违反，而在失败代价高或不安全的领域，这种滞后可能为时已晚。

Method: 构建两阶段验证流水线：首先用 CSP 模型作为对观测事件轨迹的一致性约束门，立即拒绝不符合模型的行为；随后将同一 CSP 模型翻译为 Buchi 自动机，用以约束预测性 LTL 监控器所探索的未来。据此把与模型一致的轨迹前缀分类为：已保证满足、已必然违反或仍不确定。文中形式化了组合监控器并说明其实现。

Result: 在核仓库巡检机器人巡视器的案例研究中，CSP 验证与预测性 LTL 的结合能够比标准运行时监控给出更早的判定，同时复用了已有的设计期 CSP 模型。

Conclusion: 将 CSP 一致性检查与预测性 LTL 验证相结合，可在保持对模型外行为即时拒绝的同时，对模型内轨迹提前做出满足或违反的判定，并复用现有设计期模型，从而缓解传统运行时验证的反应式滞后问题。

Abstract: Runtime Verification is well suited to autonomous and robotic systems because it checks the behaviour that is actually observed during execution. Its main limitation, however, is that it is usually reactive: the monitor detects a violation only after the system has already performed a bad event. This can be too late in domains where failures are costly or unsafe. In this paper we present PREDICTIVE VARANUS, a two-stage verification pipeline that combines VARANUS, a runtime verifier that uses models written in the process algebra Communicating Sequential Processes (CSP), with predictive runtime verification for LTL. A CSP model is first used as a conformance gate over the observed event trace; the same model is then translated into a Buchi automaton that constrains the futures explored by a predictive LTL monitor. In this way, out-of-model behaviour is rejected immediately, while model-consistent prefixes can be classified as already guaranteeing satisfaction, already forcing violation, or still being inconclusive for the monitored temporal property. We formalise the combined monitor, explain its implementation, and illustrate the approach on a robotic rover for nuclear-store inspection. The case study shows how the combination of CSP validation and predictive LTL can provide earlier verdicts than standard runtime monitoring while reusing an existing design-time CSP model.

</details>


### [289] [Flexible-body Modeling, Kinematic Identification, and Assembly Accuracy of Overconstrained Spatial Linkages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17627)
*Daniel Huczala,Michael Pieber,Johannes Gerstmayr,Andreas Mair,Frederik Schulte,Silvia Glas,Tomas Postulka,Ales Vysocky,Martin Pfurner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于浮动参考系公式的柔性多体建模框架，对过约束单环连杆机构进行整体精度分析，并以3D打印Bennett四杆机构（含故意轴错位变体）验证，发现此类机构具有"自装配"倾向：结构柔性驱动装配趋向理想几何构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过约束单环连杆机构高效、紧凑、低成本，但工业部署受限。原因在于：仿真中刚体公式因冗余约束而失效，且关于装配后机构实际精度的整体分析缺失。

Method: 采用基于浮动参考系公式（FFRF）的柔性多体建模框架；对3D打印PLA的Bennett四杆机构原型（含故意关节轴错位变体）进行验证；提出适用于病态雅可比矩阵机构的运动学参数辨识方法；并用纸管与竹棒搭建低成本机构作定性验证。

Result: 实验与仿真结果对比表明：过约束机构表现出自装配倾向，结构柔性使装配趋向理想几何构型，并将约束应力分散至整个结构；纸管与竹棒搭建的机构证明了低精度、非常规材料亦可实现功能性机构。

Conclusion: 该建模流程完全算法化，为过约束机构的设计优化奠定基础，并拓展了其在低成本工业场景中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Overconstrained rational single-loop linkages are efficient, compact, and low-cost custom mechanisms, yet their deployment in industrial settings is limited. In simulations, rigid body formulations fail due to redundant constraints. This study presents a flexible multibody modeling framework based on the floating frame of reference formulation, and delivers an overall accuracy analysis of assembled linkages prototypes. The approach is validated against 3D-printed PLA prototypes of a Bennett four-bar mechanism, including variants with intentional joint-axis misalignment, which theoretically, from the rigid body point of view, cannot be assembled. A supplementary contribution is delivered in the form of a kinematic parameter identification methodology suited for this type of mechanism with ill-conditioned Jacobian. The experimental and simulation results are compared and reveal that these overconstrained mechanisms exhibit a self-assembling tendency -- structural compliance drives the assembly toward the ideal geometric configuration, distributing constraint stress throughout the structure. Additional qualitative demonstrations using cardboard tubes and bamboo sticks as link building blocks confirm that functional mechanisms can be realized from low-cost, unconventional materials with limited manufacturing accuracy. The proposed modeling pipeline is fully algorithmic and enables design optimization in the future.

</details>


### [290] [LEAP: Learning Emergent Active Perception for Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17628)
*Ü. Bora Gökbakan,Stéphane Caron,Philippe Souères*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LEAP方法，无需额外任务目标即让导航策略自发涌现主动感知（注视控制），在危险地形目标导航中成功率92.7%，优于脚本式74.2%与被动感知34.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体主动选择视角可减少环境不确定性，但现有学习系统依赖人工设计的代理目标（如覆盖率、好奇心奖励），可能与任务目标冲突。作者希望在不修改任务目标的前提下，让主动感知自发涌现。

Method: 将问题形式化为危险地形上的目标导向导航（目标需视觉发现）；提出带主动感知的导航策略架构，在仅靠任务压力的地形课程上训练；核心是注视不变表示，将深度图像整合进以自我为中心的信念地图。

Result: 在留出评测场景中成功率达92.7%，对比脚本式74.2%、被动感知34.5%，并接近特权oracle仅差4.6个百分点；LEAP导航策略原样可直接用于物理仿真中四足运动策略的转向控制。

Conclusion: 无需代理奖励，仅靠任务压力即可涌现注视控制式主动感知；注视不变表示是关键，且策略具备跨任务迁移能力。

Abstract: Active perception allows autonomous agents to select their viewpoints rather than passively process the viewpoints given to them, enabling them to target where to reduce uncertainty about their environment. Learned systems typically encourage this behavior with hand-designed proxy objectives, such as coverage or curiosity bonuses, that may conflict with the task. In this work, we propose a method to learn emergent active perception (LEAP) without augmentation of the task objective. We formulate the problem of goal-oriented navigation over hazardous terrains with goals that must be discovered visually. We then propose an architecture for navigation policies with active perception, and train them on a terrain curriculum where task pressure alone leads to the emergence of gaze control. Key to this emergence, LEAP works on a gaze-invariant representation that integrates depth images into egocentric belief maps. We validate its performance in held-out evaluation scenarios, where it achieves a 92.7% success rate, compared to 74.2% for scripted or 34.5% for passive perception, and comes within 4.6 points of a privileged oracle. We validate that LEAP navigation policies, unchanged, can be directly applied to steering quadrupedal locomotion policies in physics simulation.

</details>


### [291] [Vision-Language Grounded Task-Context-Aware Imitation Learning for Robotic Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17714)
*Jeon Ho Kang,Igal Tamarkin,Ethan Niu,Ian Novales,Satyandra K. Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出结合语言任务上下文与分层任务选择的模仿学习框架，用于真实场景下的长时序机器人拆解任务，显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界机器人拆解需要长时序执行，机器人在单一场景中需对多个零件按顺序完成操作任务。由于存在多个有效任务目标和多样的装配配置，仅凭原始观测，模仿策略难以推断出预期技能，尤其当训练数据无法覆盖真实配置与零件几何的组合多样性时。

Method: 提出一个框架，将分层任务选择与任务上下文感知的模仿学习相结合，通过语言引入任务上下文，为技能选择提供显式结构，并将语言指定的任务与视觉场景中对应的操作目标关联，从而把语言指令落地到空间视觉表示中。该方法无需显式物体标注。

Result: 该方法可泛化到多种连接件几何形状和装配配置，无需显式物体标注。端到端任务成功率相比基线扩散策略提升35个百分点，相比此前的任务上下文感知基线提升75个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过语言引入任务上下文，可有效缓解技能选择与目标关联的困难，使机器人拆解策略在多样几何与装配配置下具备更好的泛化能力与任务成功率。

Abstract: Real-world robotic disassembly requires long-horizon execution, where robots must perform ordered sequences of manipulation tasks across multiple parts within a single scene. Multiple valid task goals and diverse assembly configurations make it difficult for imitation policies to infer the intended skill from raw observations alone, particularly when training data cannot cover the combinatorial diversity of real-world configurations and part geometries. We show that incorporating task context through language alleviates these challenges by providing explicit structure for skill selection and associating language-specified tasks with their corresponding manipulation targets in the visual scene. The proposed framework combines hierarchical task selection with task-context-aware imitation learning to ground language instructions in spatial visual representations for robotic disassembly. The resulting framework generalizes across diverse connector geometries and assembly configurations without requiring explicit object annotations. Our method improves end-to-end task success by 35 percentage points over the baseline diffusion policy and by 75 percentage points over the previous task-context-aware baseline.

</details>


### [292] [RAF-VLA: Representation Alignment with the Future for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17728)
*Dogun Kim,Yongjae Lee,Joonhee Lim,Yeina Lee,Junhyeok Park,Moogeun Park,Dongsuk Kum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RAF-VLA 框架，通过将策略隐藏状态与未来帧表征对齐（而非显式生成未来图像）来塑造规划相关内部表征，在 NAVSIM 上以极少训练开销（3.8%）和可忽略推理开销（1 ms）取得与 SOTA VLA 规划器相当的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶 VLA 模型通过显式预测未来驾驶场景（世界建模）来提供密集监督，从而学习对规划有用的内部表征，但显式未来生成带来两个关键限制：额外训练负担和推理时延。因此需要一种无需生成未来帧、却能获得同等表征指导的方法。

Method: 提出 RAF-VLA（Representation Alignment with the Future），采用未来对齐监督微调（Future-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning）：在学习驾驶动作的同时，用一个简单的正则化项将策略的隐藏状态与预训练世界编码器输出的未来帧表征对齐，从而以直接指导替代显式未来生成。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 基准上的大量实验表明，RAF-VLA 在训练样本所见数量大幅减少的情况下，规划性能与最先进的 VLA 规划器相当；同时仅带来 3.8% 的训练开销和可忽略的 1 ms 推理开销。

Conclusion: 通过表征对齐而非显式未来生成来引入未来信息，可在避免生成带来的训练与推理成本的同时有效塑造规划相关表征，为自动驾驶 VLA 提供了一种高效且性能有竞争力的方案。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving have incorporated world modeling by predicting future driving scenes alongside driving actions, demonstrating strong planning performance. Future driving scenes are utilized as dense supervision, encouraging the policy to learn rich internal representations useful for planning. However, these World-Modeling VLAs rely on explicit future generation to learn such representations, thereby introducing two key limitations: additional training burden and inference latency. To address these limitations, we propose RAF-VLA (Representation Alignment with the Future), a VLA-based autonomous driving framework that shapes planning-relevant internal representations through direct guidance from future-frame representations. RAF-VLA employs Future-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning, in which a straightforward regularization aligns the policy's hidden states with future-frame representations obtained from a pretrained world encoder while learning driving actions. This simple alignment allows RAF-VLA to avoid the training burden and inference latency associated with future generation. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark show that RAF-VLA achieves competitive planning performance against state-of-the-art VLA planners with substantially fewer training samples seen. Moreover, RAF-VLA incurs only 3.8% training overhead and a negligible 1 ms inference overhead.

</details>


### [293] [CALOS: Control-Affine Lyapunov On-manifold Safety Layer for Safe Deep Reinforcement Learning for Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17758)
*Fabrizio Cesareo,Sebastiano Mengozzi,Nicola Mimmo,Andrea Acquaviva*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CALOS，一种运行期安全层，通过求解二次规划对四旋翼标称力矩施加最小范数修正，实现姿态约束的实时强制满足，且不改变底层强化学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在四旋翼控制中表现出色，但所学策略在训练与部署阶段均无法保证满足安全约束，因此需要一种不侵入学习算法的运行期安全保障机制。

Method: CALOS将四个倾角不等式与Lyapunov下降条件统一建模为单个二次规划，以策略标称力矩输出的最小范数修正为解；该规划在三维力矩空间中通过有效集枚举精确求解，计算成本低，可支撑数千并行仿真环境下的实时约束执行。

Result: 在NVIDIA Isaac Lab的轨迹跟踪任务中，CALOS相比无约束PPO基线将横向跟踪误差降低55-60%，并在训练轨迹上实现零姿态约束违规；同时通过将探索限制在安全区域，加速训练收敛并提升数据效率，且未产生次优策略。

Conclusion: CALOS能够在保持学习算法不变的前提下，以低成本实时方式为四旋翼策略提供姿态安全保证，并兼得性能提升与训练效率改善，适用于大规模并行深度强化学习训练。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning has demonstrated remarkable capability in quadrotor control, yet learned policies offer no guarantee of respecting safety constraints during training or deployment. We present CALOS (Control-Affine Lyapunov On-manifold Safety), a runtime safety layer that enforces attitude constraints on a quadrotor without modifying the underlying learning algorithm. CALOS formulates four tilt-angle inequalities and a Lyapunov descent condition as a single quadratic program whose solution is the minimum-norm correction to the nominal torque output of the policy. The quadratic program is solved exactly via active-set enumeration over the three-dimensional torque space, with a computational cost low enough to enforce constraints in real time across thousands of parallel simulation environments, as required by modern massively parallel Deep Reinforcement Learning training. Evaluated on trajectory-tracking tasks in NVIDIA Isaac Lab, CALOS reduces lateral tracking error by 55-60% relative to an unconstrained Proximal Policy Optimization baseline while achieving zero attitude-constraint violations on the training trajectory. By restricting exploration to safe regions of the state space, the safety layer also accelerates training convergence and improves data efficiency without producing suboptimal policies.

</details>


### [294] [DRT&R: Direct Radar Teach & Repeat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17766)
*Alexander Krawciw,Daniil Lisus,Cedric Le Gentil,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DRT&R，一种基于旋转雷达的直接导航栈，通过结合直接雷达处理与局部建图，在公路与越野环境中实现SOTA定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达导航对雨雪、尘雾等恶劣条件具有鲁棒性，可弥补激光雷达的不足。现有直接法虽提升了公路全局定位性能，但尚未应用于越野环境和闭环系统；且全局地图会平滑掉依赖视角的雷达特征，损失可用于定位的姿态信息。

Method: 提出Direct Radar Teach & Repeat (DRT&R)：一种直接旋转雷达导航栈，将直接雷达处理与局部建图相结合，以最大化保留信息量。

Result: 使用344 km公路数据和20 km越野数据，在多数条件下定位误差小于4 cm，在几何退化与稀疏环境中为12 cm。使用Clearpath Warthog越野车配合MPC控制器进行超10 km自主闭环测试，证明可实时运行并达到越野雷达导航SOTA跟踪性能。

Conclusion: DRT&R在公路与越野环境中均取得SOTA定位与跟踪性能，验证了直接雷达方法在越野与闭环导航中的有效性。

Abstract: Radar-based navigation is appealing for its robustness to adverse conditions involving airborne particles, such as precipitation, dust, fog, and smoke, that can cause lidar-based systems to fail. Recently, direct methods that retain and use the entire radar scan rather than sparse points have improved on-road global localization performance. However, they have yet to be deployed in off-road environments or in closed-loop systems. Additionally, even direct global maps may lose information: their global nature leads to a smoothing out of viewpoint-dependent radar artifacts, which can provide pose information when mapping and localization occur along similar trajectories. This paper introduces Direct Radar Teach & Repeat (DRT&R): a direct spinning radar-based navigation stack that maximizes the amount of retained information by combining direct radar processing with local mapping. DRT&R yields state-of-the-art (SOTA) localization performance in both on-road and off-road environments. Using 344 km of on-road data and 20 km of off-road data, DRT&R is able to localize to within 4 cm in most on-road and off-road conditions, and 12 cm in geometrically degenerate and sparse environments. DRT&R is also evaluated autonomously in closed loop with an MPC controller for more than 10 km using a Clearpath Warthog off-road vehicle, demonstrating that it runs in real time and achieves SOTA tracking performance for off-road radar navigation.

</details>


### [295] [HINT-Plan: Human Intention-Aware Robot Task Planning in Context-Rich Environments using Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17771)
*Yuchen Liu,Luigi Palmieri,Lujun Li,Radu State,Ilche Georgievski,Marco Aiello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HINT-Plan将人类意图预测融入机器人任务规划，利用视觉语言模型从第三人称图像推断高层人类意图并转化为目标状态，借助层次化场景图进行联合任务规划，在仿真中取得69.71%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将人类意识纳入移动机器人决策的方法多聚焦低层运动规划中的碰撞避免，忽视了人类存在及高层行为带来的挑战，缺少将人类意图融入机器人任务规划的研究。

Method: HINT-Plan使用视觉语言模型（VLMs）从第三人称图像观测中预测高层人类意图，将其转化为目标状态并求解联合任务规划问题；同时采用层次化场景图（SGs）作为环境的高层表示，以实现丰富上下文环境中的场景感知，并将环境拓扑与可操作知识转换为形式化规划语言，确保计划可执行。

Result: 在照片级真实感仿真中，HINT-Plan在联合人机任务规划上取得69.71%的总体成功率，较基线最高提升35.29%，同时减少了功能性冲突。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式地将推断出的人类意图纳入形式化多智能体任务规划，对于实现主动的人类感知机器人决策是有效的。

Abstract: Approaches to incorporating human awareness into mobile robot decision-making mainly focus on collision avoidance in low-level motion planning, often overlooking the challenges posed by human presence and high-level behavior. To address this vacancy, we present HINT-Plan, a novel approach to integrate human intention prediction into robot task planning. HINT-Plan employs Vision Language Models (VLMs) to anticipate high-level human intentions from third-person image observations, convert them into goal states, and solve joint task-planning problems. To effectively enable scene awareness in context-rich environments, we use hierarchical Scene Graphs (SGs) as high-level representations of the environment, and translate environmental topology and actionable knowledge into formal planning language to ensure executable plans. Evaluated in a photorealistic simulation, HINT-Plan achieves an overall success rate of 69.71% in joint human-robot task planning, substantially outperforming the baselines by up to 35.29%, while also reducing functional conflicts. The results show the effectiveness of explicitly incorporating inferred human intentions into formal multi-agent task planning for proactive human-aware robot decision-making.

</details>


### [296] [SPROUT: The Open-Source Soft Growing Robot for Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17781)
*Antonio Alvarez Valdivia,Ciera McFarland,Robert Reeve,Ankush Dhawan,Chad Council,Megan Richardson,Margaret McGuinness,Nathaniel Hanson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SPROUT（Soft Pathfinding Robotic Observation Unit），一套面向搜救场景的可复现、可靠的软体机器人系统，并公开了代码、零件清单、CAD和搭建指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人长期被视为搜救理想方案，但从实验室走向现场存在显著门槛，缺乏可复现、可靠的系统。

Method: 与专业城市搜救团队多年互动迭代设计，采用模块化硬件与基于ROS 2的软件栈，支持任务专用载荷与控制功能，并提供补充材料以供社区复现。

Result: 在应急救援人员使用的倒塌结构训练场地中，完成多种高难度检查与通过任务；系统适应粉尘、潮湿和孤立环境。

Conclusion: SPROUT可在搜救之外扩展至基础设施检查、考古等应用，推动软体机器人从实验室走向实地部署。

Abstract: Soft robotic systems have long been theorized as ideal candidates for use in search and rescue operations; however, there have been significant barriers to entry in graduating soft robotic systems from the laboratory to the field. To address this gap in replicable, reliable soft robot systems, we present the designs for SPROUT, the Soft Pathfinding Robotic Observation Unit. The system has matured over years of interaction with professional urban search and rescue communities, with the goal of operating in dusty, wet, and isolated conditions. This manuscript contains supplementary material, including code, parts manifests, CAD assemblies, and build instructions to allow the broader robotics research community to build their own SPROUT systems. The modular hardware and ROS 2-based software stack support task-specific payloads and control functions, allowing SPROUT to be adapted for applications beyond search and rescue, including infrastructure inspection and archaeology. Here, we demonstrate SPROUT performing a variety of challenging inspection and traversal tasks in collapsed structure training sites used by first responders. The main project page is available online at https://sprout-mitll.github.io/sprout/.

</details>


### [297] [Learning Multi-Humanoid Pickup and Transport via Decentralized Object-Centric Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17824)
*Bikram Pandit,Mohitvishnu S. Gadde,Aayam Kumar Shrestha,Alan Fern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化的、以物体为中心的控制方法，使不同规模的人形机器人团队能协作抓取与搬运尺寸、重量、几何各异 的物体，且无需为每项任务重新设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作搬运不同大小、重量与几何形状的物体需要不同规模的机器人团队。传统方法往往需要针对每项任务单独设计控制器，缺乏通用的控制抽象。

Method: 采用去中心化的物体为中心控制：为每个机器人分配共享物体上的局部附着区域，通过无夹爪的双臂捏取学习实现抓取与搬运。该基于附着区域的接口提供统一控制抽象，覆盖单机器人抓取、多机器人协作搬运及机器人间传递，无需逐任务重设计。

Result: 仅在单机器人抓取任务上训练的策略已能非平凡地迁移到协作场景；显式多机器人训练进一步提升性能。在仿真中跨不同团队规模和物体几何进行验证，并实现从仿真到硬件的迁移，使真实人形机器人完成协作操作任务。

Conclusion: 该附着式抽象捕捉了协作所需的大量结构信息，同时共享物体耦合带来的协调动态值得直接学习；方法在仿真与真实硬件上均有效。

Abstract: We study cooperative multi-humanoid pickup and transport of objects with varying size, weight, and geometry, requiring robot teams of different sizes. Our approach uses decentralized object-centric control, where each humanoid is assigned a local attachment region on the shared object and learns to realize pickup and transport through gripperless bimanual pinching. This attachment-based interface provides a common control abstraction spanning single-robot pickup, cooperative multi-robot transport, and robot-to-robot handover, without per-task redesign. We find that policies trained only on single-robot pickup already transfer nontrivially to cooperative settings, suggesting that this abstraction captures much of the structure needed for coordination. At the same time, explicit multi-robot training further improves performance, showing that shared-object coupling introduces coordination dynamics that are beneficial to learn directly. We validate the approach in simulation across varying team sizes and object geometries, and demonstrate sim-to-real transfer on hardware, where the learned controllers enable real humanoids to perform cooperative manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [298] [Adaptive-MHE : A Sampling-Based Adaptive MPC for Legged Loco-Manipulation via Moving Horizon Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17832)
*Hossein Keshavarz,Alejandro Ramirez-Serrano,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Adaptive-MHE，一种基于滚动时域估计(MHE)的在线采样式系统辨识框架，可实时估计物体与地形的物理参数（如质量、摩擦），并与采样式模型预测控制器耦合，实现变化与不确定环境下的自适应运动-操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人的运动-操作行为生成困难，核心难点在于物体与地形参数对机器人未知，且与仿真参数不匹配会带来sim-to-real差距，降低控制性能。经典系统辨识假设动力学可微，而接触丰富的足式系统不满足该假设；采样式系统辨识虽规避了可微性要求，但现有方法多为离线应用，无法随环境变化自适应。

Method: 提出Adaptive-MHE，一个基于滚动时域估计(MHE)的在线采样式系统辨识框架。它通过大规模并行rollout直接匹配仿真与记录的状态轨迹，在线估计环境中物体与地形的物理参数（如质量、摩擦），并将该估计与采样式模型预测控制器相耦合。

Result: 在仿真与硬件实验中，该框架持续优于基线方法，并达到与使用真值参数的控制器相当的性能。

Conclusion: 在线采样式系统辨识与采样式MPC的耦合可有效应对参数未知与环境变化，缩小sim-to-real差距，实现自适应运动-操作。

Abstract: Legged robots have demonstrated a remarkable ability to traverse various terrains, yet generating effective loco-manipulation behaviors remains challenging. A key difficulty is that object and terrain parameters are typically unknown to the robot, and mismatches between these parameters and their simulated counterparts introduce a sim-to-real gap that degrades control performance. Classical system identification (Sys-ID) methods often assume differentiable dynamics, an assumption that does not hold for contact-rich legged systems. Sampling-based Sys-ID avoids this restriction by directly matching simulated and recorded state trajectories through massively parallel rollouts, but existing approaches are typically applied offline and do not adapt as environmental conditions change. We present Adaptive-MHE an online sampling-based Sys-ID framework, based on moving horizon estimation (MHE), that estimates the physical parameters of objects and terrain in the environment (e.g., mass, friction) and couples this estimate with a sampling-based model predictive controller, enabling adaptive loco-manipulation in changing and uncertain environments. In simulation and hardware experiments, our framework consistently outperforms baselines and matches the performance of a controller with access to ground-truth parameters.

</details>


### [299] [VCTP: Vehicle-Conditioned Terrain Planning for Off-Road Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17834)
*Akshay Naik,Ramavarapu S. Sreenivas,Dustin Nottage,Ahmet Soylemezoglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出车辆条件地形规划（VCTP），通过在不同航向下评估共享高程与地表ID层，结合车身几何与车轮接触约束，实现顾及车辆朝向的前向/反向路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆航向会同时影响轮胎下方地表以及车身俯仰与横滚，但现有地形规划往往忽略这一耦合关系，因此需要一种保留航向、车身几何与车轮接触之间关系的方法。

Method: VCTP在八个航向下评估共享的高程与地表ID层；用车身几何约束可通行性，用加载车轮接触确定建模地表代价及预测俯仰/横滚；基于D* Lite与车辆状态搜索规划含前向和反向运动的路线；当观测变化时，重新计算所有受影响的车身或接触查询。

Result: 在完全观测的双轨仿真中，于车轮接触处采样相比车辆中心采样使建模地表代价降低39.3%，同时缩短路线。在RGator记录的离线规划中，VCTP相对以距离为中心的规划降低了已识别地表与观测支撑上的建模代价，但不完全覆盖使全程路线排序尚未解决。在474次对比中，选择性更新与完全重计算结果一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明车轮接触位置与车辆航向确实会影响路线选择，VCTP能够刻画这种影响并支持高效增量更新。

Abstract: A vehicle's heading affects both the surfaces beneath its tires and its pitch and roll. We present Vehicle-Conditioned Terrain Planning (VCTP), which retains these relationships by evaluating shared elevation and surface-ID layers at eight headings. Body geometry constrains admissibility, while loaded wheel contacts determine modeled surface cost and predicted pitch and roll. Established D* Lite and vehicle-state search use these evaluations to plan routes with forward and reverse motion. When observations change, VCTP recomputes every affected body or contact query. In fully observed two-track simulations, sampling at wheel contacts rather than at the vehicle center lowers modeled surface cost by 39.3% while shortening the route. In offline planning on RGator recordings, VCTP also reduces modeled costs over identified surfaces and observed support relative to distance-focused planning, although incomplete coverage leaves full-route rankings unresolved. Selective updates match full recomputation in all 474 comparisons using recorded map changes. These results identify when wheel-contact placement and vehicle heading affect route choice.

</details>


### [300] [Timely Activation of Safety Filters via One-Step Reachability Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17904)
*Javier Borquez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对离散采样下最小限制安全滤波器因连续时间假设失效的问题，提出基于单步扩展BRT的解决方案，通过提前触发安全干预保证离散执行下的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Hamilton-Jacobi可达性的最小限制安全滤波器仅在系统到达不安全状态集（BRT）边界时才覆盖名义控制器，从而提供强安全保证。但这些保证依赖连续时间公式。实际机器人系统在离散采样间隔施加控制，导致系统可能在两次更新之间跳入不安全BRT，产生理论上可避免的失效。因此需要一种在离散时间执行下仍能保证正确性的安全滤波方法。

Method: 作者提出基于"单步扩展BRT"的原则性方案，该集合预测在一个时间步内能够到达真实BRT的所有状态。将这一扩展边界作为安全滤波器的激活条件，使安全干预足够提前。作者将该扩展集合形式化为一个修改后的可达性问题，并使用标准连续时间求解器进行计算。

Result: 使用扩展边界作为激活条件后，安全干预发生得足够早，从而确保在离散时间执行下的正确性。扩展集合可通过标准连续时间求解器计算得到。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了连续时间安全保证与离散时间机器人执行之间的不匹配，通过单步扩展BRT实现离散执行下的正确安全滤波，且计算上兼容现有连续时间可达性求解工具。

Abstract: Least-restrictive safety filters based on Hamilton-Jacobi reachability provide strong safety guarantees by overriding a nominal controller only when the system reaches the boundary of the set of unsafe states defined as a Backward Reachable Tube (BRT). These guarantees, however, rely on the continuous-time nature of the underlying formulation. In practice, robotic systems apply control at discrete sampling intervals, which creates a mismatch where the system may jump into the unsafe BRT between updates, allowing failures that are theoretically avoidable. This work introduces a principled solution based on a one-step expanded BRT that predicts all states capable of reaching the true BRT within a single timestep. By using this expanded boundary as the activation condition for the safety filter, safety interventions occur early enough to ensure correctness under discrete-time execution. We formulate this expanded set as a modified reachability problem and compute it using standard continuous-time solvers.

</details>


### [301] [Map2Route: Benchmarking Compositional Language-Grounded Route Planning over Semantic Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17910)
*Muyi Bao,Hang Xu,Jingfan Tang,Zihan Liu,Yuxin Cai,Chen Lv,Wenshan Wang,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Map2Route基准与Grounding2Route方法，用于语义地图上的组合式语言路径规划，性能优于现有方法但仍有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅部分测试了基于语言、结合语义地图的路径规划能力，缺少一个系统化、覆盖多种复杂指令类型的评测基准。

Method: 构建Map2Route人工标注基准，包含40个场景、1000个episode，指令涉及关系、比较、嵌套描述，并包含必经过区域、必避区域、五类软偏好及时空/阶段范围；同时提出Grounding2Route方法，融合可执行代码式grounding、验证引导修复与范围感知规划。

Result: 在七个代表性适配基线上，Grounding2Route在所有指标上均显著优于现有方法；但与人类示范仍有明显差距。

Conclusion: Map2Route具有较高难度，Grounding2Route表现更优但仍未达到人类水平，未来仍有较大研究空间。

Abstract: We introduce Map2Route, a human-curated benchmark for compositional language-grounded route planning over pre-built semantic maps. Map2Route contains 1,000 episodes across 40 scenes, where instructions use relational, comparative, and nested descriptions to identify route-relevant objects and regions, while specifying ordered must-pass regions, must-avoid requirements, five categories of soft preferences, and spatial and route-stage scopes, which is partially tested by existing works. Alongside Map2Route, we propose Grounding2Route, which combines executable code-as-grounding with verification-guided repair and scope-aware planning.Across seven representative adapted baselines, Grounding2Route substantially outperforms existing methods in all metrics. Despite these gains, a substantial gap to human demonstrations remains, highlighting the difficulty of Map2Route and the considerable headroom for future progress. Additional qualitative results and resources are available on https://anonymous.4open.science/w/Map2Route-F05F/.

</details>


### [302] [Multi-Session Multimodal Underwater Mapping with Acoustic and Optical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17929)
*Precious Philip-Ifabiyi,Valerio Franchi,Fausto Ferreira,Nuno Gracias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于因子图优化的多航次、多模态水下建图框架，联合优化轨迹、地标、传感器外参与航次间对齐，在真实数据集上提升地图一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海底精确建图对海洋科学、考古和环境监测至关重要，但不同传感器（侧扫声呐与光学相机）在不同调查航次采集的数据，因定位漂移和传感器外参偏差难以融合。

Method: 采用因子图优化框架，联合优化载具轨迹、三维地标位置、传感器外参以及每个航次的全局对齐变换；结合刚性航次间校正与局部轨迹形变，同时补偿航次间偏移和航次内累积导航误差导致的畸变。

Result: 在加泰罗尼亚海岸真实数据集上验证，所有指标（含像素精度和平均交并比）均优于未优化和刚性对齐基线；像素精度较未优化基线提升3.4%，对应约14700平方米测绘区域的语义标注改善；定性结果显示在显著轨迹畸变和航次间错位下，声呐与光学地图仍能一致配准。

Conclusion: 该框架能够从异构水下调查数据生成连贯的多模态海底地图，展现出良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate seafloor mapping is essential for marine science, archaeology, and environmental monitoring. However, integrating data from different sensors, such as side-scan sonar and optical cameras, collected across separate survey sessions, remains challenging due to positioning drift and sensor offsets. This paper presents a multi-session, multimodal underwater mapping framework based on factor graph optimization. The method jointly optimizes vehicle trajectories, 3D landmark positions, sensor extrinsics, and per-session global alignment transformations. By combining rigid inter-session corrections with local trajectory deformations, it compensates for both inter-session offsets and intra-session distortions from accumulated navigation errors. The proposed methodology was validated on real-world datasets collected along the Catalan coast. Results show measurable improvements in map consistency over both unoptimized and rigid-alignment baselines across all metrics, including Pixel Accuracy and mean Intersection over Union. The method achieves a 3.4% improvement in pixel accuracy over the unoptimized baseline, corresponding to improved semantic labelling across approximately 14700 $\text{m}^2$ of mapped area. Qualitative results further show consistent co-registration between sonar and optical maps, even in the presence of significant trajectory distortions and inter-session misalignments. These findings demonstrate the potential of the proposed framework to generate coherent multimodal seafloor maps from heterogeneous underwater surveys.

</details>


### [303] [Feedback-Modulated Harmonic Policies for Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17946)
*Yixuan Jia,Steven Roche,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将每条关节轨迹表示为受指令条件约束的傅里叶级数，并由上下文网络生成系数与反馈网络权重，在线调整关节偏移、谐波增益、频率和相位，在仿真与Unitree Go2上验证了显式频率结构的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型四足运动策略通常直接将观测映射到关节级动作，使运动的周期结构隐含在策略内部，缺乏可解释性和显式的频率表征。

Method: 提出指令条件下的傅里叶级数关节轨迹表示：上下文网络生成傅里叶系数及逐步反馈网络权重，反馈输出在线调整关节偏移、谐波增益、频率与相位；在仿真中对比显式频率结构与直接输出关节目标的MLP策略隐激活，并对MLP轨迹做动态模式分解（DMD）分析。

Result: 谐波波形随指令速度改变频率与形状；DMD显示MLP主导激活模态集中在足高振荡频率及其二次谐波附近，表明即使无显式傅里叶生成器也存在周期结构；Unitree Go2上仿真训练的谐波控制器实现约3.67 m/s的板载估计峰值速度，并分别承载最高5.883 kg负载。

Conclusion: 显式傅里叶表示可有效刻画四足运动的周期结构，并与隐式学习的MLP策略共享相似的频率特征，且具备实机高速与负载能力。

Abstract: Learned quadruped locomotion policies commonly map observations directly to joint-level actions, leaving the periodic structure of locomotion implicit in the policy. We investigate an alternative representation in which each joint trajectory is expressed as a command-conditioned Fourier series and modified online using feedback from the robot state. A context network generates the Fourier coefficients and the weights of a per-step feedback network, whose outputs adjust joint offsets, harmonic gains, frequency, and phase during execution. In simulation, we examine this explicit frequency structure alongside the hidden activations of an MLP policy that directly outputs joint targets. The harmonic waveforms change frequency and shape with commanded speed. Dynamic mode decomposition of selected MLP rollouts reveals dominant activation modes near the foot-height oscillation frequency and its second harmonic, showing periodic structure without an explicit Fourier generator. On a Unitree Go2, the simulation-trained harmonic controller records a provisional onboard-estimated peak speed of 3.67 meter per second and carries added loads up to 5.883 kilogram in separate trials.

</details>


### [304] [Causal-History Test-Time Scaling for Failure Recovery in Autoregressive World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18016)
*Lin Li,Long Chen,Kwunhang,Wong,Jiaming Lei,Song Jin,Shucheng Du,Chuhan Zhang,Songchen Ma,Weihao Zhang,Jun Xiao,Kwang-Ting,Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的框架，通过测试时缩放因果历史实现机器人操作中的失败恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型主要基于成功轨迹训练，当真实执行偏离学习动态时容易失败，自回归模型中误差会进入因果历史并持续影响后续预测。

Method: 将失败恢复重新表述为因果历史上的测试时缩放，分解为三个耦合决策（何时修订、从何处恢复可靠前缀、选择哪种历史配置），并通过三个阶段实现：进度感知触发、历史前缀恢复、假设验证。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作设置中任务成功率均有提升，消融实验确认各恢复阶段的贡献。

Conclusion: 该训练无关框架有效缓解了世界-动作模型的失败恢复问题，提升了执行鲁棒性。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot manipulation by jointly modeling future visual dynamics and robot actions. However, existing WAMs are trained predominantly on successful trajectories, making them prone to failure when real-world execution diverges from the learned dynamics. This issue is amplified in autoregressive WAMs, where execution errors become part of the causal history and continue to influence subsequent predictions. To this end, we introduce \method{}, a training-free framework that reformulates failure recovery as \emph{test-time scaling over causal histories}. This formulation decomposes recovery into three coupled decisions: \emph{when} to revise the causal history, \emph{where} to recover a reliable history prefix, and \emph{which} history configuration best supports subsequent execution. Specifically, \method{} realizes these decisions through three stages: 1) \textbf{Progress-Aware Recovery Trigger} detects persistent non-progress and triggers recovery only when the current execution state permits intervention; 2) \textbf{History-Prefix Recovery} identifies the unreliable history suffix, retrieves a historical anchor matching the current physical state, and reconstructs the causal KV state from the retained prefix while conditioning on the latest real observation; and 3) \textbf{Hypothesis Verification} compares the future continuations induced by complete-history, recovered-prefix, and full-reset hypotheses, and commits the best-supported hypothesis. Experiments in both simulated and real-world manipulation settings demonstrate consistent improvements in task success, while ablations confirm the contribution of each recovery stage.

</details>


### [305] [Online Multimodal Workload Assessment in Contact-Rich Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18031)
*Yanyi Chen,Fan Yang,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在线多模态交互负荷评估框架，融合交互力/力矩、平面TCP运动学与皮肤电导水平，并用路径曲率和任务进度调节各因素权重，在24名被试18种环境组合下验证，与生理参考达中等相关且具可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集型物理人机交互中，交互、运动调节和生理响应带来的需求随时间变化，因此需要连续的交互负荷评估；同时现有方法（如学习类基线）缺乏透明性和可解释性。

Method: 构建在线多模态评估框架，将交互力/力矩（wrench）、平面工具中心点（TCP）运动学和皮肤电导水平（SCL）整合为四个可解释的负荷相关因素；利用路径曲率反映运动需求变化，利用任务进度反映生理随时间漂移，动态调整各因素相对贡献。在两种导纳控制模式下，对24名被试在温度、声噪、照度18种受控组合中进行评估。采用严格留一被试（LOSO）评估，以标准化瞳孔直径（PDz）作为独立生理参考，并与静态变体和代表性学习类SOTA基线对比。

Result: 框架与生理参考的队列平均30秒分块Spearman相关为ρ30=0.308，24名被试中23名呈正相关；整体性能与学习类SOTA基线相当；优于对应的固定权重形式；在两种导纳控制模式下表现一致。

Conclusion: 该框架通过显式负荷因素和明确加权规则保持评估过程透明可解释，同时取得与SOTA相当的性能，支持在接触密集型pHRI中实现透明、可解释的连续交互负荷评估。

Abstract: Contact-rich physical human--robot interaction (pHRI) imposes time-varying demands associated with physical interaction, motor regulation, and physiological response, motivating continuous assessment of interaction workload. This paper presents an online multimodal assessment framework that integrates interaction wrench, planar tool-center-point (TCP) kinematics, and skin conductance level (SCL) into four interpretable workload-related factors. Their relative contributions are adjusted using path curvature to reflect changes in motion demand and task progression to account for gradual physiological variation over time. The framework was evaluated with 24 participants across 18 controlled combinations of temperature, acoustic noise, and illuminance under two admittance-control modes. Strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) evaluation used standardized pupil diameter ($\mathrm{PD}_z$) as an independent physiological reference and included comparisons with static variants and representative state-of-the-art learning-based baselines. The proposed framework achieves a cohort-mean $30\,\mathrm{s}$ block-wise Spearman correlation of $ρ_{30}=0.308$ with the physiological reference, with positive subject-level correspondence in 23 of 24 participants. Its overall performance is comparable to the state-of-the-art learning-based baseline. At the same time, our framework keeps the assessment process transparent through explicit workload-related factors and defined weighting rules, while outperforming the corresponding fixed-weight formulation. The framework also maintains consistent performance across the two tested admittance-control modes. These results support a transparent and interpretable approach to continuous interaction workload assessment in contact-rich pHRI.

</details>


### [306] [Geometric Shortcuts for Complex Trunk Postures: Dual-Helicity Coupling Enables Low-Dimensional Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18050)
*Huishi Huang,Danlu Chen,Matteo Lo Preti,Jun Liu,Marcelo H. Ang,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 大象鼻子的复杂姿态可能源于对侧手性斜肌之间的"双螺旋耦合"这一低维几何捷径；在简化软体机器人原型中，仅改变两个几何参数即可生成丰富的大象式姿态库，提示存在双层控制架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大象鼻子如何在不过度依赖精细的逐段肌肉激活的情况下生成复杂姿态，并借此为连续体机器人设计提供思路与生物学假说。

Method: 提出"双螺旋耦合"假说：由左右手性相反的斜肌相互耦合构成低维几何捷径；并构建简化的软体机器人原型，仅调节两个几何参数来验证姿态生成能力。

Result: 仅通过改变两个几何参数，原型即可生成大量类似大象鼻子的姿态，形成丰富的姿态库。

Conclusion: 大象鼻子的姿态复杂性可能部分来自低维几何机制而非全靠精细分段激活，提示一种双层控制架构，对连续体机器人设计与生物学假设均有启示。

Abstract: How do elephant trunks generate complex postures without relying solely on fine segmental activation? We propose that part of this complexity arises from a low-dimensional geometric shortcut: dual-helicity coupling between opposite-handed oblique muscles. In a simplified soft-robotic prototype, varying only two geometric parameters generates a broad library of elephant-like postures, suggesting a dual-layer control architecture with implications for continuum robot design and biological hypotheses.

</details>


### [307] [CLASP: A Cluster-Level Autonomous Selective Picking Robot with a Soft Rolling-Band Gripper for Fresh-Market Blueberry Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18051)
*Yixuan Xia,Yilin Cai,Natalia Belen Espinoza,Changying Li,Zilfina Rubio Ames,Xin Zhang,Yue Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CLASP——一种簇级自主选择性采摘机器人，配备软体主动滚带夹爪（SARB-Gripper），通过双柔性带包络果实簇并滚动、闭环调控拉力，实现成熟浆果的顺序脱除，在端到端田间试验中自主抓取成功率达92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲜食蓝莓需要选择性、轻柔采摘，人工成本高；跨行机械采收快但非选择性，混熟果实被损伤，只能用于加工市场；现有选择性机器人多针对单果而非果簇，对小型密集簇生蓝莓效率低。因此需要一种面向果簇的选择性采摘方案。

Method: 提出CLASP机器人及SARB-Gripper：用两条柔性带包络果簇并与果实滚动接触，将成熟浆果依次拽下；通过拉力闭环调控使施加负载低于未熟果实的脱落阈值。感知采用全局—局部流水线：手眼分离（eye-to-hand）相机负责全局果簇检测与目标选择，眼在手上（eye-in-hand）相机负责局部定位与果簇朝向估计。

Result: 田间测量证实成熟与未成熟果实间存在清晰的脱落力差异；SARB-Gripper可将指令拉力复现误差控制在3.7%以内，实现簇级选择性采摘。端到端田间试验中，CLASP自主抓取25个果簇中的23个，成功率92%；单台组件成本约3326美元。

Conclusion: CLASP以较低成本实现了鲜食蓝莓的簇级选择性采摘，具备可扩展性，为选择性簇级采收提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Fresh-market blueberries require selective, gentle picking, which is labor-intensive and expensive. Over-the-row machine harvesters are fast but non-selective, bruising mixed-ripeness fruit and limiting yield to the processing market. Selective robotic harvesters typically target individual fruits rather than fruit clusters, which limits harvesting efficiency for small, densely clustered blueberries. This paper presents CLASP, a Cluster-Level Autonomous Selective Picking robot with a Soft Active Rolling-Band Gripper (SARB-Gripper). Two compliant bands envelop the cluster and roll against the fruit, drawing mature berries off in sequence, while closed-loop regulation of the pulling force keeps the applied load below the immature detachment threshold. A global-to-local perception pipeline pairs an eye-to-hand camera for global cluster detection and target selection with an eye-in-hand camera for local localization and cluster orientation estimation. Field measurements confirm a clear detachment-force separation between mature and immature fruit, and the SARB-Gripper reproduces a commanded pulling force to within \SI{3.7}{\percent}, enabling selective harvesting at the cluster level. In end-to-end field trials, CLASP autonomously grasped 23 of 25 presented clusters (\SI{92}{\percent}). With the component cost of approximately \$3326 per unit, CLASP offers a scalable approach to selective cluster-level harvesting for fresh-market blueberries.

</details>


### [308] [An Efficient Algorithm for Minimum-Pressure Growth Planning of Vine Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18070)
*Andres C. Torres,Tobia Marcucci,Elliot W. Hawkes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究藤蔓机器人在多面体障碍物间生长的最小压力路径规划问题，提出保证全局最优（2D）和近似最优（3D）的高效算法，并用仿真与硬件实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 藤蔓机器人从尖端延伸以穿越杂乱环境，但生长规划（寻找最优生长路径）研究不足；已有规划器未考虑跟随路径所需的生长压力，压力过高会导致机器人爆裂。

Method: 首先推导任意形状藤蔓机器人的生长压力方程，证明总存在一条仅在障碍物特定点处弯折的分段线性最小压力路径；进而将生长规划问题归约为带时变权值的最短路径问题，并用改进的 Dijkstra 算法高效求解。

Result: 数值仿真展示了方法的速度与可扩展性，硬件实验验证了算法有效性，并提供了开源高性能 Python 实现 VinePlanner。

Conclusion: 该算法在 2D 中保证全局最优、在 3D 中给出误差随离散化参数趋于零的近似解，解决了藤蔓机器人最小压力生长路径规划问题。

Abstract: Vine robots navigate cluttered environments by extending from their tip. Although their ability to operate in such environments has been extensively demonstrated, little work has addressed growth planning, i.e., finding optimal growth paths. Moreover, existing planners do not account for the growth pressure necessary to follow a given path, which can cause the robot to burst when it is too high. In this paper, we address the problem of finding minimum-pressure paths for vine robots growing around polytopic obstacles. We propose an efficient algorithm that is guaranteed to find globally optimal solutions in 2D and approximate solutions in 3D, with an error that vanishes as a discretization parameter approaches zero. First, we derive a growth pressure equation for vine robots of arbitrary shape, which we use to show that there always exists a minimum-pressure path that is piecewise-linear and can bend only at specific points on the obstacles. We then leverage this observation to reduce the growth-planning problem to a shortest-path problem with time-dependent weights, which we efficiently solve using a modified Dijkstra's algorithm. We demonstrate the speed and scalability of our approach through numerical simulations. We also validate our algorithm with hardware experiments and provide an open-source and high-performance implementation in the Python package, VinePlanner: https://github.com/Ahsoka/VinePlanner.

</details>


### [309] [Characterizing Refraction-Induced Ranging Bias in Underwater Collaborative Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18073)
*Timothy Kogucki,Alan Papalia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过千米尺度多智能体仿真，量化了折射引起的声学测距偏差对协同定位精度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体测距辅助导航是大尺度水下定位的有前景方案，但精度依赖测距质量。声速变化会导致声线折射弯曲，而传感器融合算法为可解性假设直线传播，这种折射会使测距系统性偏长。该偏差在千米尺度多智能体协同定位中的影响此前尚未被研究。

Method: 开展一系列多智能体千米尺度仿真实验：使用HYCOM再分析数据重建真实海洋环境，用射线追踪生成含折射的测距，并采用集中式多智能体因子图估计器量化测距偏差对轨迹估计的影响。

Result: 初步结果表明，折射引起的偏差可导致估计轨迹的显著退化，在声速梯度剧烈的区域尤为明显。

Conclusion: 折射偏差在千米尺度多智能体协同定位中不可忽视，需在算法中加以考虑；仿真环境已开源以支持后续研究。

Abstract: This work studies how refraction-induced bias on acoustic ranging affects multi-agent collaborative localization in a range of oceanographic conditions and spatial scales. While multi-agent range-aided navigation, which uses range measurements to either fixed infrastructure or other agents, is a promising solution to the challenges of large-scale underwater localization, its accuracy depends strongly on the quality of range measurements. Sound speed variability induces refraction (bending) of acoustic rays, yet, for algorithmic tractability, standard sensor fusion pipelines assume straight-line propagation. This refraction systematically biases range measurements to be longer than the straight-line assumption predicts. However, the effects of this bias on multi-agent collaborative localization on kilometer scales remains unexplored. We present a series of simulated experiments with several agents operating over kilometer scales. The simulation uses HYCOM reanalysis data to recreate realistic oceanographic conditions, ray tracing to generate refraction-informed ranges, and a centralized multi-agent factor graph estimator to quantify the resulting measurement bias on estimated trajectories. Preliminary results indicate that refraction-induced bias can induce significant degradation of estimated trajectories, particularly in regions with sharp sound-speed gradients. We also share the simulation environment to support further studies https://github.com/UMich-RobotExploration/manta-ray.

</details>


### [310] [Not All Layers Need Tuning: Diagnosing and Directing Adaptation in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18084)
*Shahram Najam Syed,Arthur Jakobsson,Prayuj Sachdev,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文测量了VLA模型各区域的适应成本，发现其呈结构化分布，并据此提出"观察-诊断-分配-适应"流水线，用变秩LoRA适配器实现高效微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为VLA模型在新部署环境微调成本高昂，但现有方法对所有网络区域统一施加相同容量的适配器，隐含假设每个区域需要同等调整。论文旨在检验这一假设，并利用适应成本的结构性差异提升微调效率。

Method: 在五种架构各异的VLA（OpenVLA-OFT、π0、SmolVLA、DTP、Octo，93M-7B参数）上，以区域隔离微调下的归一化参数位移度量各区域适应成本。提出"观察-诊断-分配-适应"流水线：从10个无标注目标观测、无需微调，通过无参考梯度、蒙特卡洛Dropout信号与缓存源参考的CKA分数估计各区域成本；分配器在参数预算下生成变秩LoRA适配器并冻结校准良好的区域；再用标准LoRA微调训练。

Result: 诊断在每次部署中对区域排序的中位Spearman为0.91；在LIBERO和CALVIN上所有测试预算下分配方案达到或超过统一LoRA。在物理xArm-7上，指令措辞偏移场景下以0.04%可训练参数匹配全量微调；在五个未重新训练的留出场景中领先所有基线，30次rollout中成功11-23次，最强参数高效基线为8-18次，全量微调为2-11次。

Conclusion: VLA的适应成本具有足够结构，可在微调开始前被测量并加以利用，从而实现更高效的参数分配与跨场景泛化。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model for a new deployment environment is expensive, yet most methods apply uniform-capacity adapters to every network region as if every region requires equal adjustment. This paper tests that assumption on five architecturally diverse VLAs (OpenVLA-OFT, $π_0$, SmolVLA, DTP, Octo; 93M-7B parameters). Measuring per-region adaptation cost as normalized parameter displacement under region-isolated fine-tuning reveals an adaptation spectrum in which appearance shifts concentrate cost in the vision encoder, instruction shifts in the language backbone, and novel-object shifts in the vision encoder together with the action head, across all five architectures. To exploit this structure, we introduce a pipeline that observes, diagnoses, allocates, and adapts. From ten unlabeled target observations and without fine-tuning, the diagnostic estimates per-region cost by combining reference-free gradient and Monte Carlo Dropout signals with a Centered Kernel Alignment score against a cached source reference; the allocator converts the estimates into variable-rank LoRA adapters under a parameter budget and freezes well-calibrated regions; and standard LoRA fine-tuning trains the resulting adapters. The diagnostic ranks regions within each deployment at a median Spearman of 0.91, and the allocation matches or exceeds uniform LoRA at every budget we tested on LIBERO and CALVIN. On a physical xArm-7, the pipeline matches full fine-tuning under an instruction-wording shift with 0.04% of its trainable parameters, and on five held-out scenes evaluated without retraining it leads every baseline, with 11-23 successes of 30 rollouts against 8-18 for the strongest parameter-efficient baseline at equal or larger budgets and 2-11 for full fine-tuning. These results suggest that adaptation cost in VLAs is structured enough to measure before fine-tuning begins.

</details>


### [311] [ReRadar: Robust Radar Global Localization via Rotation-Equivariant Descriptor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18092)
*Duc Manh Nguyen,Truong Giang Dao,Gia Nghiem Luong,Viet Trung Hoang,Anh Quang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReRadar，一种基于旋转等变特征与旋转不变描述子的毫米波雷达全局定位流水线，通过描述子检索结合地标匹配估计3-DoF位姿，在多个数据集上取得高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描式毫米波雷达全局定位仍具挑战，因为位置识别描述子往往丢弃了精确位姿检索所需的空​​间结构信息。

Method: 使用可转向卷积神经网络提取旋转等变中间特征，通过群池化与NetVLAD聚合形成旋转不变描述子，并将描述子检索与基于地标的匹配相结合以估计3自由度位姿。

Result: 在固定数据库-查询评估中，经目标数据集自适应的ReRadar在OORD Bellmouth达99.37% Recall@1，Mulran DCC01达91.44% Recall@1且F1_max为80.99%，falling-snow Boreas序列达99.38% Recall@1；无目标数据集时跨数据集模型在OORD达98.07% Recall@1，与所评估的先进方法相当。

Conclusion: ReRadar能有效保留空间结构信息，实现高精度雷达全局定位，并在有无目标域数据条件下均表现优异。

Abstract: Global localization with scanning millimeter-wave radar remains challenging because place-recognition descriptors often discard spatial structure needed for accurate pose retrieval. We present ReRadar, a radar global localization pipeline that extracts rotation-equivariant intermediate features using steerable convolutional neural networks, forms rotation-invariant descriptors through group pooling and NetVLAD aggregation, and combines descriptor retrieval with landmark-based matching to estimate the robot's three-degree-of-freedom (3-DoF) pose. Across fixed database-query evaluations, ReRadar with target-dataset adaptation achieves 99.37% Recall@1 on OORD Bellmouth, 91.44% Recall@1 with 80.99% F1_max on Mulran DCC01, and 99.38% Recall@1 on falling-snow Boreas sequence. Without target-dataset data, the cross-dataset model reaches 98.07% Recall@1 on OORD, performing comparably to the evaluated state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [312] [Beyond Pixel Similarity: Task-Aware Evaluation of GAN-Based Synthetic Sonar Data for Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18100)
*Hannan Ejaz Keen,Muhammad Moazam Fraz,Karsten Berns*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究GAN生成的合成声呐图像中，传统图像保真度指标是否充分反映下游感知性能，发现像素级指标与目标检测性能之间存在不一致，主张采用任务感知评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据可降低机器人感知训练数据的采集与标注成本，但生成能保留下游感知相关特征的传感器观测（尤其声呐图像）仍具挑战。作者质疑传统图像保真度指标是否足以反映合成声呐数据的下游感知表现。

Method: 采用Pix2Pix条件生成对抗网络，配置四种不同感受野的判别器：PixelGAN、PatchGAN-16、PatchGAN-70和ImageGAN。模型在两个数据集的声呐图像上训练，先用SSIM、PSNR、MSE等传统保真度指标评估；再用仅在真实声呐图像上训练的YOLOX-S、YOLOX-L和Faster R-CNN检测器，在相同测试样本与标注下对GAN生成图像进行检测评估，作为任务导向评价。

Result: 结果揭示图像保真度与下游目标检测性能之间存在差异：在SSIM、PSNR、MSE上表现最佳的配置并不总能带来最佳检测性能。特别是PatchGAN配置虽然像素级相似度得分并非最高，却取得了较强的下游检测结果。

Conclusion: 在所考虑的数据集与模型范围内，仅靠像素级图像保真度指标可能无法稳定捕捉合成声呐观测中与任务相关的真实感。因此，面向机器人感知的合成传感器数据，应引入任务感知的评估方式。

Abstract: Synthetic data can reduce the cost of collecting and annotating training data for robotic perception, but generating sensor observations that preserve the characteristics relevant to downstream perception remains challenging, particularly for sonar imagery. In this work, we investigate whether conventional image-fidelity metrics adequately reflect the downstream perception performance of GAN-generated synthetic sonar data. We employ a Pix2Pix conditional generative adversarial network with four discriminator configurations characterized by different receptive fields: PixelGAN, PatchGAN-16, PatchGAN-70, and ImageGAN. The models are trained using sonar imagery from two datasets and evaluated using conventional image-fidelity metrics, including Structural Similarity Index (SSIM), Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR), and Mean Squared Error (MSE). To complement these pixel-level measures with task-oriented evaluation, YOLOX-S, YOLOX-L, and Faster R-CNN detectors are trained exclusively on real sonar imagery and subsequently evaluated on the GAN-generated images using identical test samples and annotations across all discriminator configurations. The results reveal a discrepancy between image-fidelity and downstream object-detection performance: the configuration achieving the best SSIM, PSNR, and MSE does not consistently yield the best detection performance. In particular, PatchGAN configurations achieve strong downstream detection results despite not achieving the highest pixel-level similarity scores. These findings suggest, for the datasets and models considered, pixel-level image-fidelity metrics alone may not consistently capture the task-relevant realism of synthetic sonar observations and motivate the use of task-aware evaluation for synthetic sensor data intended for robotic perception.

</details>


### [313] [Technical Report: One-Step Drifting Action Heads for GR00T N1.7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18108)
*Xihe Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将GR00T N1.7的迭代扩散动作头替换为一步drift动作头可大幅降低推理耗时（动作头45.3ms→5.0ms，整体70.0ms→30.6ms），但LIBERO任务成功率系统性下降，呈明显速度-成功率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步动作生成能显著降低VLA策略的推理成本，但其对闭环任务成功率的影响尚不明确，需要实证研究。

Method: 在GR00T N1.7变体中将迭代扩散Transformer动作头替换为一步drift动作头，并加入面向异步chunk替换的overlap条件扩展；多随机种子drift实验在两张NVIDIA A800上训练与评测。

Result: LIBERO上动作头前向时间约45.3ms降至5.0ms，backbone+动作头约70.0ms降至30.6ms；三个drift种子成功率：Spatial 64.0±4.0%，Goal 52.0±1.0%，Long 26.0±2.6%，种子方差小。

Conclusion: 该结果应被视为速度-成功率权衡而非常规性能提升；成功 degrad 可能源于确定性一步模式平均、依赖batch的几何估计、长开环chunk执行，以及同步LIBERO评测未覆盖异步overlap路径。

Abstract: One-step action generation can substantially reduce the inference cost of vision-language-action (VLA) policies, but its effect on closed-loop task success remains an open question. This technical report studies a GR00T N1.7 variant in which the iterative diffusion-transformer action head is replaced by a one-step drifting action head, together with an overlap-conditioned extension for asynchronous chunk replacement. All multi-seed drifting runs were trained on two NVIDIA A800 GPUs. On LIBERO, the action head reduces the mean model-forward time of the action head from approximately $45.3\,\mathrm{ms}$ to $5.0\,\mathrm{ms}$, while the measured backbone-plus-head time falls from approximately $70.0\,\mathrm{ms}$ to $30.6\,\mathrm{ms}$. However, this speedup is accompanied by a systematic reduction in task success. Across three drifting seeds, success is $64.0\pm4.0\%$ on LIBERO-Spatial, $52.0\pm1.0\%$ on LIBERO-Goal, and $26.0\pm2.6\%$ on LIBERO-Long. The low seed variance indicates that the degradation is not explained by random initialization alone. We report the result as a speed--success trade-off rather than an overall improvement, and discuss likely contributing factors including deterministic one-step mode averaging, batch-dependent geometry estimation, long open-loop chunk execution, and the fact that synchronous LIBERO evaluation does not exercise the asynchronous overlap path.

</details>


### [314] [A Comprehensive Review of Generative Physical Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18111)
*Satyam Gaba,Krutiksinh Rana,Siva Sai,Vinay Chamola,Dusit Niyato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了生成式物理人工智能（GPAI）系统，提出涵盖机器人基础模型、视觉-语言-动作模型、大行为模型、扩散策略模型和世界基础模型五大类别的分类体系，分析其架构、应用与局限，并展望数据高效学习、仿真到现实迁移、边缘适配架构与安全框架等方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基础模型与物理实体结合推动了机器人领域进步，形成能自主感知、推理与行动的GPAI系统。为系统理解这类系统的架构基础、当前应用与关键局限，需要一份全面的综述与分类框架，以指导具身智能在物联网环境中的发展。

Method: 采用综述与分类学方法，提出五类GPAI方法：机器人基础模型（RFMs）用于跨平台技能迁移；视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）用于端到端多模态感知与控制；大行为模型（LBMs）用于类人运动生成；扩散策略模型（DPMs）用于基于扩散模型的时间连贯动作生成；世界基础模型（WFMs）用于物理合规的仿真与数据生成。并分析这些方法之间的互补关系。

Result: 通过自动驾驶、工业自动化、医疗机器人和人形系统等应用实例，识别出显著的性能提升，并总结出数据高效学习、仿真到现实迁移、边缘兼容架构和安全框架等有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: 该综述为物联网连接环境中智能体与网络化传感器、执行器和边缘设备交互的具身AI提供了系统洞见，推动GPAI与具身智能的进一步发展。

Abstract: The integration of large-scale foundation models with physical embodiments has led to significant advancements in robotics known as Generative Physical Artificial Intelligence (GPAI). These agentic AI systems autonomously perceive, reason, and act in complex real-world situations. This survey comprehensively analyzes GPAI systems, focusing on their architectural foundations, current applications, and key limitations. We introduce a taxonomy of five distinct approaches: Robot Foundation Models (RFMs) for cross-platform skill transfer; Vision-Language Action (VLA) models for end-to-end multi-modal perception and control; Large Behavior Models (LBMs) for human-like movement generation; Diffusion Policy Models (DPMs) for diffusion model-based temporally coherent action generation; and World Foundation Models (WFMs) for physics-compliant simulation and data generation. We examine how these approaches complement each other: WFMs generate training data for VLAs and DPMs, RFMs enable cross-platform deployment of learned policies, while LBMs provide motion priors for natural behavior. Through examples across autonomous vehicles, industrial automation, healthcare robotics, and humanoid systems, we identify significant performance improvements and summarize promising research directions in data-efficient learning, sim-to-real transfer, edge-compatible architectures, and safety frameworks. These insights advance embodied AI for IoT-connected environments where intelligent agents interact with networked sensors, actuators, and edge devices.

</details>


### [315] [OpenDexGrasp: Open-vocabulary Task-Oriented Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18117)
*Jiyao Zhang,Junhan Wang,Tianyu Wang,Zeyuan Chen,Anthony Bolten,Yitong Peng,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出开放词汇任务导向灵巧抓取的数据与生成建模框架 OpenDexGrasp，通过 Coverage-to-Alignment 配方构建双源监督数据集 OpenDexVerse，学习耦合视觉-语言与动作的共享隐表示，实现任务一致的灵巧抓取直接生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取合成虽能生成稳定且物理合理的姿态，但真实操作需要保持任务所隐含的功能意图。开放词汇任务导向灵巧抓取要求机器人从自由形式语言推断功能意图，并将其落地到多视图视觉和物体几何上，生成可执行的高自由度抓取，这一设定尚未被统一解决。

Method: 提出统一的数据与生成建模框架 OpenDexGrasp。构建 OpenDexVerse 数据集，采用 Coverage-to-Alignment (C2A) 配方组织双源监督：OpenDex-Scale 通过自动抓取合成与视觉-语言标注提供大规模语义与几何覆盖；OpenDex-Align 通过人类遥操作与类别级迁移提供高质量具身对齐。模型学习共享的感知-动作隐表示，将开放词汇视觉-语言上下文与灵巧动作生成耦合，用可供性落地和抓取生成在该隐空间上提供互补监督。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人实验中，方法在功能对齐、物理可行性、对未见类别的泛化以及真实世界执行成功率上均有提升。无需独立的可供性到姿态推断阶段即可直接生成任务一致的灵巧抓取。

Conclusion: OpenDexGrasp 为开放词汇任务导向灵巧抓取提供了统一的数据与生成建模方案，兼顾功能一致性与物理可行性，并具备跨类别泛化与真实执行能力。

Abstract: Dexterous grasp synthesis has advanced rapidly in generating stable and physically plausible hand poses, but real-world manipulation requires grasps that preserve the function implied by the task. We study open-vocabulary task-oriented dexterous grasp generation, where a robot must infer functional intent from free-form language, ground it in multi-view visual observations and object geometry, and generate an executable high-degree-of-freedom grasp. We present OpenDexGrasp, a unified data and generative modeling framework for this setting. OpenDexVerse provides dual-source supervision organized by the Coverage-to-Alignment (C2A) Recipe: OpenDex-Scale offers large-scale semantic and geometric coverage through automatic grasp synthesis and vision-language annotation, while OpenDex-Align supplies high-quality embodied alignment through human teleoperation and category-level transfer. OpenDexGrasp learns a shared perception-action latent representation that couples open-vocabulary vision-language context with dexterous action generation. Affordance grounding and grasp generation provide complementary supervision over this latent space, enabling direct generation of task-consistent dexterous grasps without a separate affordance-to-pose inference stage. Extensive simulation and real-robot experiments demonstrate improved functional alignment, physical feasibility, generalization to unseen categories, and real-world execution success. Additional details and videos are available at https://opendexgrasp.github.io/.

</details>


### [316] [Fetch My Beer: Synthetic-to-real Hierarchical Policy for Smooth Pick-and-place](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18119)
*Yingyue Li,Chenyangguang Zhang,Ruida Zhang,Bowen Fu,Guangyao Zhai,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出合成到真实的框架，结合物理验证的数据生成与分层扩散控制器，实现液体容器稳定运输，抑制晃动并防止溢出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态敏感操作要求在整个执行过程中维持物体动态稳定，而现有系统存在三大局限：流体仿真用于在线强化学习过于昂贵；人类遥操作在模仿学习中引入非预期加速度导致晃动；当前策略流程仅优化任务完成而非动态稳定。因此需要研究液体容器稳定运输这一任务。

Method: 合成到真实（synthetic-to-real）框架，包含两部分：1）可扩展数据管线，合成抓取、用视觉语言模型过滤不稳定姿态、通过流体仿真验证运输轨迹；2）分层扩散策略，高层模块将语言与视觉观测转换为SE(3)控制目标，潜在扩散控制器先在紧凑潜在空间高效规划，再解码密集动作块，以实现平滑稳定所需的高控制频率。

Result: 大量实验表明，该系统在运输平滑度和动态稳定性上优于当前最先进的操作策略。

Conclusion: 该工作通过物理验证的数据生成与分层扩散控制器的结合，有效解决了液体容器稳定运输中的晃动与溢出问题，验证了合成到真实框架在动态敏感操作任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Many real-world robotic applications require dynamically sensitive manipulation, where success depends not only on reaching a target state but on maintaining stable object dynamics throughout execution. We study the stable transport of liquid-filled containers, where a robot must move objects to target locations while suppressing sloshing and preventing spillage. Unlike conventional pick-and-place, this task imposes stringent requirements on motion smoothness and trajectory-level stability, exposing clear limitations in existing systems. Specifically, fluid simulation remains too costly for online reinforcement learning; human teleoperation introduces unintended accelerations that induce sloshing during imitation learning; and current policy pipelines optimize for task completion rather than dynamic stability. We propose a synthetic-to-real framework coupling physically validated data generation with a hierarchical, diffusion-based controller. The scalable data pipeline synthesizes grasps, filters unstable poses via a vision-language model, and validates transport trajectories through fluid simulation. The policy is organized with a high-level module that translates language and visual observations into SE(3) control targets, and a latent diffusion controller that first plans efficiently in a compact latent space and then decodes dense action chunks, enabling the high control frequency needed for smooth and stable motion. Extensive experiments show our system outperforms state-of-the-art manipulation policies in transport smoothness and dynamic stability. Our project page: https://fetch-my-beer.github.io/

</details>


### [317] [Prior Evolution and Task Alignment for Aerial Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18153)
*Weiliang Deng,Zhengyang Dang,Yao Mu,Ximin Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出在解析规划器框架内引入学习方法，通过轨迹先验学习、CEM迭代演化和执行感知评价器，提升空中抓取轨迹优化的可靠性与实际抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中抓取轨迹优化存在两个实践局限：一是优化问题高度非凸、对初始化敏感，在有限算力预算下难以获得高质量解；二是人为设计的数值目标只是对成功抓取的有限抽象，未必能真正刻画任务成功的关键因素。

Method: 首先从已优化轨迹中学习轨迹先验；然后通过基于CEM的过程，用实际部署的优化器评估采样初始化并保留较优者作为新监督来演化先验。同时训练执行感知评价器（Execution-Aware Critic），从接触、抬升和完成结果中学习判断优化轨迹在物理执行中是否可能成功；其冻结能量还可作为可微抓取代价，使执行数据直接塑造轨迹生成。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，该方法在优化可靠性、轨迹一致性和抓取性能方面均有提升。
