Results show that DeformSmith generates assets with better visual quality and physical plausibility than state-of-the-art baselines, including PhysGen3D, PhysGM, and PhysX-Omni, while supporting the synthesis of data for robotic manipulation of deformable objects. Project page: https://can-lee.github.io/deformsmith-web/

</details>


### [352] [Benchmarking Visual-Inertial Odometry in Subterranean Environments Under Sensor Degradation, Miscalibration, and Dynamic Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18628)
*Yueying Zhu,Xiang Li,Thien-Minh Nguyen,Xuehe Wang,Shenghai Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向地下环境的VIO故障中心压力测试基准，基于CERBERUS数据集在九种扰动下评估四类VIO系统，揭示不同范式的脆弱性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VIO是GPS拒止地下环境中自主运行的核心能力，但传感器漂移、标定误差和动态遮挡会使其可靠性急剧下降。现有评估主要关注标称条件下的精度，难以揭示实际部署中何时发生失效。

Method: 基于CERBERUS数据集构建故障中心压力测试基准，系统评估四种代表性VIO系统（涵盖滤波、优化和学习三类范式），在九种实际扰动设置下测试，包括IMU偏置与噪声变化、相机内参和外参漂移、动态场景遮挡。除传统轨迹误差外，还通过覆盖率和失效阈值分析鲁棒性极限。

Result: 实验揭示了标称性能无法捕捉的失效行为，并显示不同VIO范式存在明显不同的脆弱性模式：部分方法对惯性退化更敏感，另一些则更受几何误标定或动态干扰影响。

Conclusion: 研究为地下场景中的VIO选择、标定优先级和可靠运行提供面向部署的指导，并将发布完整基准脚本和评估流程以支持可复现评估与后续扩展。

Abstract: Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) is a core capability for autonomous operation in GPS-denied subterranean environments, yet its reliability can degrade sharply under sensor drift, calibration errors, and dynamic occlusion. Existing evaluations mainly emphasize nominal-condition accuracy, offering limited insight into when practical deployment failures occur. In this work, we present a failure-centric stress-test benchmark for VIO in underground environments using the CERBERUS dataset. We systematically evaluate four representative VIO systems spanning filtering-, optimization-, and learning-based paradigms under nine practical perturbation settings, including IMU bias and noise variation, camera intrinsic and extrinsic drift, and dynamic scene occlusion. Beyond conventional trajectory error, we analyze robustness limits through coverage ratio and failure thresholds, revealing breakdown behaviors that are not captured by nominal-condition performance alone. Our study shows distinct vulnerability patterns across VIO paradigms: some methods are more sensitive to inertial degradation, while others are more affected by geometric miscalibration or dynamic interference. These results provide deployment-oriented guidance for VIO selection, calibration prioritization, and reliable operation in challenging underground scenarios. To support reproducible evaluation and future extensions, we will release the full benchmark scripts and evaluation pipeline.

</details>


### [353] [From Gameplay to Policy: Towards Scalable Robot Data Collection via Gamified Robot-Free Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18650)
*Zheng Li,Liang Zhu,Junzhe Wang,Huayuan Chen,Ziyun Liu,Jiahang Cao,Xinyu Sheng,Pei Qu,Yufei Jia,Ximeng Zhang,Jiarui Xie,Zizhao Yuan,Haoang Li,Yi Cai,Jinni Zhou,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Project Kitchen——VR游戏化第一人称数据采集平台，结合Game2Policy将游戏操作经验迁移到真实机器人，在少样本设置下显著提升操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略的泛化需要大规模多样化交互数据，但真实世界示教采集成本高、难以扩展。现有数据采集方法或依赖特定硬件而限制众包与迁移，或存在标注不完整、行为多样性不足的问题。作者受游戏能长期吸引人的特点启发，探索将数据采集变为游戏体验并迁移至真实机器人。

Method: 提出Project Kitchen：基于VR的游戏化第一人称数据采集平台，独立于特定机器人本体与硬件，可大范围部署并激发多样化目标导向操作。为弥合游戏到现实的差距，提出Game2Policy，从游戏轨迹中提取本体无关的可供性线索（接触点与子目标状态）；先在游戏数据上预训练可供性模型，再用少量真实机器人示教与下游策略联合微调。

Result: 在少样本设置下，Game2Policy使平均成功率在仿真中提升10.0个百分点，在真实机器人上提升18.3个百分点。用户研究与定量分析表明，Project Kitchen能促进多样化操作行为并提供有吸引力的数据采集体验。

Conclusion: 结果表明游戏化虚拟环境可作为可扩展的操作知识来源；平台与代码将在论文录用后开源。

Abstract: Learning generalizable robot manipulation policies requires large-scale and diverse interaction data, yet collecting real-world demonstrations remains costly and difficult to scale. Existing approaches to data collection are either dependent on specific robot hardware that limits crowdsourcing and transferability, or suffer from incomplete annotation and limited behavioral diversity. Inspired by how games sustain long-term human engagement, we explore an alternative paradigm that turns data collection into an engaging gameplay experience and transfers the resulting human manipulation experience to real robots. We present Project Kitchen, a VR-based gamified egocentric data collection platform that elicits diverse, goal-directed manipulation while remaining independent of specific robot embodiments and hardware, making it applicable to broader and potentially large-scale deployment. To bridge the game-to-real gap, we further introduce Game2Policy, which extracts embodiment-invariant affordance cues, including contact points and sub-goal states, from gameplay trajectories. An affordance model is pre-trained on game-collected data and then jointly fine-tuned with downstream policies using only a handful of real-robot demonstrations. Experiments show that Game2Policy improves average success rates by 10.0 points in simulation and 18.3 points on real robots in the few-shot setting. User studies and quantitative analyses further show that Project Kitchen promotes diverse manipulation behaviors and provides an engaging data collection experience. These results demonstrate the potential of gamified virtual environments as a scalable source of manipulation knowledge. The platform and code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [354] [FIERCE: From Generalist Robot Policies to Fast Specialists via Progress-Failure Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18651)
*Runjia Tan,Yuang Tu,Yujie Yan,Lan Yu,Xuesong Tian,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FIERCE框架：以通用机器人策略为初始化，通过统一的任务自适应"进展-失败"评估器提供反馈，实现紧凑专家策略的强化学习精调，无需持续查询通用策略、专用仿真器或人工密集奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略能提供有用的初始化，但在有限物理交互下精调紧凑专家策略需要信息量丰富的学习反馈，而现有方法依赖通用动作查询、专用仿真器或人工标注的密集奖励，成本高且受限。

Method: 设计统一的进展-失败评估器：在观测-语言表示上共享结构，包含观测进展头与动作条件潜变量预测器，后者将过去与当前预测输入因果序列头以估计任务失败。通过进展标签、偏好标签、同步指令与观测以及终止结果联合监督训练，并利用目标任务回合进行自适应与校准。固定评估器快照提供进展塑形与失败风险惩罚，配合独立验证的终止奖励，评估器与策略交替更新。

Result: 在仿真与两个接触丰富的真实任务上评估，从反馈质量、策略学习效率和部署成本三方面进行对比分析，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: FIERCE无需通用策略动作查询、专用目标任务仿真器或人工密集奖励即可完成精调，部署时仅保留紧凑专家策略，降低了部署成本，并已开源代码、模型权重与数据恢复工具。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies offer useful initialization, but refining compact specialists through limited physical interaction requires informative learning feedback. We present FIERCE, a generalist-initialized reinforcement learning framework centered on a unified, task-adaptive progress-failure evaluator. Its architecture shares an observation-language representation between an observed-progress head and an action-conditioned latent predictor whose past and current predictions feed a causal sequence head for task-failure estimation. Joint supervision from progress and preference labels, synchronized commands and observations, and terminal outcomes trains the evaluator; target-task rollouts support adaptation and calibration. Fixed evaluator snapshots provide progress shaping and failure-risk penalties alongside independently verified terminal rewards, while evaluator and policy updates alternate as new experience is collected. Refinement requires neither continued generalist action queries nor a dedicated target-task simulator or manually annotated dense rewards. Only the compact specialist is retained at deployment. The evaluation separates feedback quality, policy-learning efficiency, and deployment cost across simulation and two contact-rich real tasks. Code, model weights, and data-restoration tools are released at https://github.com/ar-mine/FIERCE.

</details>


### [355] [VLA-ULAP: Interleaving Cloud VLA Calls with Ultra-Lightweight Local Action Prediction at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18663)
*Deyu Cao,Ryuji Oi,Kosuke Matsushima,Yuxuan Pan,Ziheng Wang,Daichi Fujiki,Atsutake Kosuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLA-ULAP，用7.4M参数的超轻量本地动作预测器与远程VLA调用交错执行，大幅降低推理耗时与能耗，同时保持成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十亿参数级VLA策略在机载设备上功耗巨大，而远程推理的通信延迟又会妨碍及时响应，因此需要一种兼顾效率与响应速度的方案。

Method: VLA-ULAP将远程VLA调用与超轻量本地动作预测器（ULAP）交错执行。ULAP约7.4M参数（含冻结视觉编码器），融合当前视图、本体感觉与已执行动作历史，一次前向预测动作块；独立训练，无需VLA隐状态、在线验证或服务端往返。

Result: Jetson Orin Nano上ULAP单次推理19.9ms、0.183J，对比RTX A6000上GR00T的284.3ms、50.55J。三组仿真中移除48.8–76.7%的VLA调用，保留95.0–97.5%成功率；对比ACT省49.2%时间与51.0%能量，对比SP-VLA省77.1%时间与79.9%能量。物理SO-101实验保留95.2–100%成功率，降低47.9–58.0%时间与52.1–62.5%能量。延迟感知LIBERO-Safety中超越π0.5达11.0与15.5个百分点，且VLA调用约减半。

Conclusion: VLA-ULAP通过轻量本地预测与远程调用交错，在显著降低推理时间与能耗的同时保持高成功率，并提升动态任务表现。

Abstract: Billion-parameter vision--language--action (VLA) policies demand substantial onboard power, while communication delays in remote inference hinder timely responses. We propose VLA-ULAP, which interleaves remote VLA calls with an Ultra-Lightweight Local Action Predictor (ULAP). With approximately 7.4M parameters including the frozen vision encoder, ULAP combines current views, proprioception, and executed action history to predict chunks in one pass. Trained independently, it requires no VLA hidden states, online verification, or server round trips. On Jetson Orin Nano, ULAP takes 19.9 ms and 0.183 J per inference, compared with 284.3 ms and 50.55 J for GR00T on RTX A6000. Across three simulated base-policy/benchmark pairs, selected operating points remove 48.8--76.7\% of VLA calls while retaining 95.0--97.5\% of the baseline success rate. Against local VLA-acceleration alternatives on VLA-JEPA, ULAP uses an estimated 49.2\% less inference time and 51.0\% less GPU energy per successful episode than ACT at comparable success rates, and 77.1\% less time and 79.9\% less energy than SP-VLA at equal success rates. Physical SO-101 experiments retain 95.2--100\% of the baseline success rate across seen and held-out placements while reducing inference time by an estimated 47.9--58.0\% and inference-device energy by 52.1--62.5\%, based on successful-episode call counts and measured device costs. Faster responses also improve dynamic-task success rates: in latency-aware LIBERO-Safety simulation, VLA-ULAP exceeds $π_{0.5}$ by 11.0 and 15.5 percentage points on two tasks while approximately halving VLA calls.

</details>


### [356] [M$^3$P-R1: Reinforcement Learning for Large Language Model Guided Multi-Modal Motion Planning via MIP Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18669)
*Xingpeng Sun,Zherong Pan,Kai Cheng,Xindi Tang,Syed Talha Bukhari,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出M³P-R1，通过强化学习微调LLM，将多模态运动规划任务分解为MIP变量、约束和目标，生成可执行Python代码并借助求解器获得可验证解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态运动规划需联合推理连续运动与离散模式切换，纯离散或纯连续规划器均难以处理；MIP提供原则性框架，但面向非凸问题的可行建模通常依赖人工与领域知识，难以高效构建。

Method: M³P-R1用强化学习微调大语言模型，将M³P任务分解为MIP变量、约束与目标；模型不直接输出答案以避免幻觉，而是生成使用MIP优化库与约束接口的可执行Python代码，并以求解器结果为结果驱动奖励进行训练，学习组合模态级离散化原语与跨模态耦合约束。

Result: 模型能够为复杂M³P任务合成可执行的MIP程序，并通过求解器后端执行，获得稳健且可验证的解决方案。

Conclusion: M³P-R1展示了用LLM结合强化学习自动构建MIP建模代码的可行性，为多模态运动规划提供可执行、可验证的求解路径。

Abstract: Multi-Modal Motion Planning (M$^3$P) requires joint reasoning over continuous motions and discrete mode transitions, making it difficult to solve efficiently. For instance, a bipedal robot may walk to a target location and then use its arms to grasp an object. This scenario captures both mode transitions and continuous dynamics, yielding feasible paths that neither purely discrete nor continuous planners can handle. While Mixed-Integer Programming (MIP) offers a principled framework, constructing tractable formulations for non-convex problems is typically manual and domain-specific, especially in the approximate, discretization-based MIP regime needed for non-convex robotic tasks. We propose M$^3$P-R1, a reinforcement learning method that fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to decompose M$^3$P tasks into MIP variables, constraints, and objectives. Instead of directly outputting answers, which are often prone to hallucination, the model generates executable Python code using MIP optimization libraries and constraint interfaces. This enables solver-backed execution for robust and verifiable solutions. Trained with an outcome-driven reward against the solver, M$^3$P-R1 learns to compose modality-level discretization primitives and synthesize cross-modal coupling constraints, producing executable MIP programs for complex M$^3$P tasks.

</details>


### [357] [WeaveRL: Weaving Reconstruction into Scene-Aware Fabrics for Perceptive Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18685)
*Remo Steiner,Vikram Ramasamy,David Tingdahl,Sam Mady,Karl Van Wyk,Nathan Ratliff,David Recasens Lafuente,Soha Pouya,Tuur Stuyck,Alex Millane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GPU加速的surfel重建方法，在数千个并行仿真中从在线3D感知构建surfel几何，使基于几何织物（geometric fabrics）的强化学习策略能处理复杂碰撞密集操作任务，并实现sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可让机器人获得复杂技能，但几何复杂操作任务的策略生成仍很困难。基于避碰控制器（如geometric fabrics）学习是有前景的方向，但现有方法依赖静态、手工指定的场景表示；将主动在线3D感知集成到大规模并行RL训练中此前无法实现。

Method: 一种GPU加速方法：在主动rollout过程中，于数千个并行仿真实例中把场景重建为surfel集合。由此策略可基于传感器获取的几何而非手工指定几何进行训练。策略建立在scene-aware的surfel fabrics之上。

Result: 在碰撞密集操作任务套件上，surfel fabrics使策略能处理复杂几何场景，而基于图元的基线方法失败，同时保持sim-to-real迁移。使用scene-aware fabric训练的策略对测试时新出现的几何更具鲁棒性，在未见障碍物下的无碰撞任务完成率从35%提升至61%。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了大规模并行训练中的在线3D感知重建与基于surfel的避碰策略学习，提升了几何复杂场景的性能与对新几何的鲁棒性；作者开源重建系统、训练代码和测试数据集以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning allows robots to acquire complex skills, but producing policies for geometrically complex manipulation remains difficult. A promising approach is to learn on top of collision-avoidant controllers, such as geometric fabrics. However, these approaches have relied on static, hand-specified representations of the scene. Integrating active, online 3D perception into massively parallel RL training has so far been inaccessible. We introduce a GPU-accelerated method that reconstructs the scene as a collection of surfels across thousands of parallel simulation instances during active rollouts. This lets policies operate over sensor-derived, rather than hand-specified, geometry. On a suite of collision-dense manipulation tasks, our surfel fabrics enable policies to tackle geometrically complex scenes where primitive-based baselines fail, while maintaining sim-to-real transfer. Furthermore, policies learned with a scene-aware fabric are more robust to the introduction of novel geometry at test time, improving collision-free task completion under unseen obstacles from 35% to 61%. We release our reconstruction system, training code and test dataset to spur research in this direction.

</details>


### [358] [Toward 3D Printable Non-Planar Electroadhesive Structures for Active Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18700)
*Mostafa A. Atalla,Carol R. Derla,Pep Canyelles Pericàs,Gijs Krijnen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文探索了利用多材料3D打印制造带有3D打印电极图案的非平面结构，用于电粘附。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电粘附垫多为平面结构，限制了其在复杂非平面部件上的应用，需要一种能将电极与结构一体化制造的方法。

Method: 采用多材料3D打印技术，将导电叉指电极作为结构的一部分打印，制作平面电粘附垫作为基准，圆柱形垫作为非平面演示，并进行法向力测量。

Result: 两种几何结构均表现出电压控制的粘附调制；在3 kV下，平面垫产生约0.11–0.13 N，圆柱形垫在5 N预载下约达0.16 N。

Conclusion: 研究展示了制造具有内置电控粘附与摩擦的打印部件的可行性，超越了传统平面电粘附垫。

Abstract: This paper investigates multi-material 3D printing as a method to fabricate non-planar structures with 3D-printed electrode patterns for electroadhesion. We printed flat electroadhesion pads as a planar benchmark and cylindrical pads as a non-planar demonstration, with conductive interdigitated electrodes 3D printed as part of the structure. Normal-force measurements showed voltage-controlled modulation in both geometries. At 3 kV, the flat pads generated approximately 0.11-0.13 N, while the cylindrical pads reached approximately 0.16 N at 5 N preload. These results demonstrate a step toward printed parts with built-in, electrically controlled adhesion and friction, beyond conventional planar electroadhesion pads.

</details>


### [359] [Calibrated Probabilistic Obstruction Reasoning with Vision-Language Models for Grasping in Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18718)
*Thanh-Tuan Tran,Ngoc-Chien Chu,Thanh Nguyen Canh,Nak Young Chong,Nguyen-Viet Ha,Xiem HoangVan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CPOR-Grasp，一个校准的概率阻塞推理框架，将成对证据的不确定性传播到动作决策，实现认证决策、自适应停止与原则性延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从杂乱环境中抓取目标需要决定抓取目标、移除阻塞物还是延迟。现有方法通常固定单一阻塞图或移除策略，忽略场景多种解释间的不确定性；同时依赖未校准的VLM预测，成对阻塞关系可能不一致，且近似方法无法保证丢弃假设对最终决策的影响。

Method: CPOR-Grasp校准并融合VLM、深度与amodal掩码线索估计阻塞概率，诱导有效阻塞图上的分布，并对图进行边缘化以计算目标可达或某阻塞物应被移除的似然。为使推理可解，仅保留最高概率图，并推导被丢弃概率质量的总变差上界，从而支持认证决策、自适应停止与原则性延迟。

Result: 在合成与真实UNOBench场景中优于现有最优基线。在Gemini Robotics骨干上校准误差从0.1416降至0.0185；图截断在99.74%的决策上与精确推理一致，且使用少56倍的图。真实实验中平均成功率达77.8%，超过SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 通过概率化、可校准的阻塞推理并给出截断误差保证，CPOR-Grasp在保持决策可靠性的同时大幅降低推理成本，并在合成与真实抓取任务上取得领先性能。

Abstract: Retrieving a target from clutter requires deciding whether to grasp the target, remove a blocker, or defer. Existing methods typically commit to a single obstruction graph or removal strategy, ignoring uncertainty across alternative scene interpretations. They also rely on miscalibrated vision-language model (VLM) predictions and can produce pairwise obstruction relations that are jointly inconsistent. Moreover, current approximations provide no guarantees about the impact of discarded hypotheses on the final decision. We propose CPOR-Grasp, a calibrated probabilistic obstruction-reasoning framework that propagates uncertainty from pairwise evidence to action decisions. CPOR-Grasp calibrates and fuses VLM, depth, and amodal-mask cues to estimate obstruction probabilities, induces a distribution over valid obstruction graphs, and marginalizes over these graphs to compute the likelihood that the target is accessible or that a given blocker should be removed. To make inference tractable, it retains only the highest-probability graphs and derives a total-variation bound on the discarded probability mass, enabling certified decisions, adaptive stopping, and principled deferral. On synthetic and real UNOBench scenes, CPOR-Grasp outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Calibration error decreases from 0.1416 to 0.0185 on the Gemini Robotics backbone, while graph truncation matches exact inference on 99.74\% of decisions using 56 times fewer graphs. In real-world experiments, CPOR-Grasp achieves a 77.8\% average success rate, surpassing SOTA baselines.

</details>


### [360] [PASSAGE: Scaling Scene-Aligned Motion Learning for Perceptive Humanoid Traversal in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18732)
*Yuxuan Ma,Zicheng Zeng,Chunlin Peng,Zhoujian Li,Zetong Zhao,Zhikai Zhang,Yunrui Lian,Han Xue,Sikai Liang,Weiyi Zhu,Mulin Chen,Chenghuai Lin,Jiayu Zeng,Yanwei An,Songan Zhang,Jiayuan Gu,Jilong Wang,Jingbo Wang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PASSAGE框架，用VR与惯性动捕采集100小时场景对齐人类运动数据，训练条件流匹配规划器与感知全身跟踪器，使类人机器人无需技能标注即可在杂乱场景中选择并组合跨越、侧身、下蹲等行为，实现全机载实时导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人虽能跨越、挤过或躲过障碍，但从机载感知中学习选择与协调这些行为仍很困难。现有方法多依赖任务专用强化学习目标或精心整理的运动库，导致行为覆盖成本高、泛化性差。作者希望用数据驱动方式，在无技能标注、无特定障碍策略的条件下获得广泛且可组合的通行能力。

Method: PASSAGE为感知条件化的规划器-跟踪器框架。数据端：用VR与惯性动捕采集100小时、覆盖1500个杂乱场景的场景对齐人类运动。规划端：条件流匹配规划器以运动历史、局部目标点和机器人中心多层高程图为输入，生成短时域参考轨迹；实时分块提升块间一致性，并在冻结跟踪器下进行规划器侧RL后训练以优化闭环性能。执行端：感知式全身跟踪器以50Hz运行并结合几何反馈执行参考动作。系统集成：机载自中心3D LiDAR感知、在线占据建图、6.25Hz规划与50Hz控制，运行于Jetson AGX Orin。

Result: 仿真中通过组件消融量化各阶段贡献。三个独立训练种子下，数据从6小时增至100小时使留出场景上的平均无障碍成功接触率从48.1%提升至68.9%，加入验证过的场景增强后最终模型达70.3%。在50个未见物理布局上，全机载系统无需预建地图或外部计算即可完成通行。

Conclusion: 单个规划器-跟踪器对即可在未见几何环境中自主选择并组合多种通行行为，证明大规模场景对齐人类运动数据加流匹配规划与感知全身跟踪的路线可有效替代技能标注和障碍专用策略，并具备全机载实时部署能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots can step over, squeeze past, and duck under obstacles, but learning to select and coordinate these behaviors from onboard perception remains challenging. Many existing approaches rely on task-specific reinforcement-learning objectives or curated motion libraries, making broad behavioral coverage costly. We present PASSAGE, a perception-conditioned planner--tracker framework for humanoid traversal. Using virtual reality and inertial motion capture, we collect 100 h of scene-aligned human motion across 1,500 cluttered scenes. A conditional flow-matching planner generates short-horizon references from motion history, a local destination, and a robot-centric multi-layer elevation map, while a perceptive whole-body tracker executes them at 50 Hz with geometric feedback. Real-time chunking promotes inter-chunk consistency, and planner-side RL post-training under the frozen tracker further improves closed-loop performance. Without skill annotations or obstacle-specific policies, one planner--tracker pair selects and composes traversal behaviors across unseen geometries. In simulation, component ablations quantify the contribution of each stage. Across three independent training seeds, scaling captured data from 6 to 100 h increases mean contact-free success from 48.1% to 68.9% on held-out scenes, while the final model with validated scene augmentation reaches 70.3%. The fully onboard system integrates egocentric 3D LiDAR perception, online occupancy mapping, 6.25 Hz planning, and 50 Hz control on a Jetson AGX Orin; tests across 50 unseen physical layouts demonstrate traversal without prebuilt maps or offboard computation.

</details>


### [361] [Active perception for robotic harvesting: 3D reconstruction and localisation of tomatoes hidden within clusters in a Mediterranean greenhouse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18738)
*Fernando Cañadas-Aránega,Rowan Border,José C. Moreno,José L. Blanco-Claraco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一套用于温室集群生长番茄的3D重建与精确定位流水线，通过主动视觉规划与多阶段点云处理，在高度遮挡条件下实现超过90%的精度，验证了自主采摘的技术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地中海温室密集农业中的机器人采摘面临植物几何结构复杂、果实被遮挡的难题。现有文献多针对孤立生长的作物（如苹果、甜椒、桃子），而集群生长蔬菜的核心挑战在于：机器人系统上固定的传感器无法探测到可见表面之后隐藏的果实，因此需要一种能够完整重建果簇几何并定位每颗果实的方法。

Method: 方法分为五个连续阶段：i) 使用AgriSEE Next Best View（NBV）主动规划器进行点云采集；ii) 通过统计离群点去除（SOR）进行随机噪声滤波；iii) 使用区域生长（RG）进行表面分类与分割；iv) 通过基于密度的噪声应用空间聚类（DBSCAN）分离并恢复被遮挡果实；v) 进行3D位姿估计（位置与朝向）。该方法可提取完整果簇几何，可靠识别部分隐藏的番茄。

Result: 在模拟框架中针对不同遮挡水平的多种场景进行评估（该框架已针对真实条件严格验证），系统精度超过90%，平均召回率为82.8%，平均交并比（mIoU）为80.7%。此外，质心估计具有高重复性，均方根误差（RMSE）仅为4.2 mm。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了其在自主采摘操作中的技术可行性与高精度，能够可靠地重建果簇几何并定位被遮挡果实。

Abstract: Automating robotic harvesting in intensive agriculture within Mediterranean greenhouses requires overcoming significant challenges related to the geometric complexity of plants and occluded fruits. Although existing literature offers solutions targeting crops that grow in isolation (e.g., apples, sweet peppers, or peaches), the fundamental challenge lies in cluster-growing vegetables, where fixed sensors mounted on robotic systems fail to detect fruits hidden behind the visible surface. To address this limitation, this study presents a comprehensive pipeline for the 3D reconstruction and precise localization of each fruit within a cluster, including heavily occluded instances. The proposed methodology is structured into five sequential stages: i) point cloud acquisition using the AgriSEE Next Best View (NBV) active planner; ii) stochastic noise filtering via Statistical Outlier Removal (SOR); iii) surface classification and segmentation using Region Growing (RG); iv) isolation and recovery of occluded fruits through Density-Based Spatial Clustering of Applications with Noise (DBSCAN); and v) 3D pose estimation (position and orientation). This approach extracts the complete cluster geometry, ensuring the reliable identification of partially hidden tomatoes. Evaluated across multiple scenarios with varying occlusion levels within a simulation framework rigorously validated against real-world conditions, the system achieves a precision exceeding 90\%, an average recall of 82.8\%, and a mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) of 80.7\%. Furthermore, it demonstrates high repeatability in centroid estimation with a Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of merely 4.2~mm, verifying its technical feasibility and high accuracy for autonomous harvesting operations.

</details>


### [362] [QMSR: Query-Conditioned Mask-wise Expert Routing for Robust Open-Vocabulary Underwater Object Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18752)
*Fuming Zhang,Dongyue Huang,Junjie Wen,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QMSR，一种查询条件化的掩码级专家路由框架，用于水下开放词汇检索，通过为每个查询-候选对自适应选择UIE专家并预测Raw-专家融合强度，NDCG@10相比共享路由器提升17.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下开放词汇目标检索在复杂环境中仍具挑战。固定式水下图像增强（UIE）策略有时甚至不如Raw表示，说明增强不应作为统一的预处理步骤；需要自适应、按需的增强方式。

Method: QMSR为每个查询-候选对选择一个预训练UIE专家，并预测连续的Raw-专家融合强度，实现自适应增强同时保留有用的Raw语义。训练时使用特权排序预言机提供专家选择与融合强度监督，并采用退火软路由松弛来优化硬Top-1路由策略。

Result: 相比图像-查询共享路由器，QMSR的NDCG@10提升17.6%，并持续优于固定UIE策略，在留出查询类别上仍有效。

Conclusion: 查询条件化且候选特定的增强路由对水下开放词汇检索是有效的。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object retrieval remains challenging in complex underwater environments. Although underwater image enhancement (UIE) can improve visual quality, fixed UIE strategies may even underperform the Raw representation in retrieval, indicating that enhancement should not be applied as a uniform preprocessing step. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{QMSR}, a query-conditioned mask-wise expert routing framework for underwater open-vocabulary retrieval. Specifically, QMSR selects one pretrained UIE expert for each query--candidate pair and predicts a continuous Raw--Expert fusion strength, enabling adaptive enhancement while preserving useful Raw semantics. During training, a privileged ranking oracle provides expert-selection and fusion-strength supervision, while an annealed soft-routing relaxation facilitates optimization of the hard Top-1 routing policy. Experiments show that QMSR improves NDCG@10 by 17.6\% over an image--query shared router, while consistently outperforming fixed UIE strategies and remaining effective on held-out query categories. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of query-conditioned and candidate-specific enhancement routing for underwater open-vocabulary retrieval.

</details>


### [363] [Gated Residual Body-Hand Coordination for Whole-Body Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18763)
*Ruiming Wu,Shuang Li,Liding Zhang,Alois Knoll,Zhaopeng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出门控残差协调框架，在冻结运动跟踪与灵巧手重定向模块的前提下施加有界修正，通过动作门与奖励门协调全身与手部几何关系，在仿真中显著降低腕部与指尖几何误差并保持全身跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身人形机器人遥操作通常将运动跟踪策略与独立的灵巧手重定向器组合使用，但两者独立生成的命令未显式保持身体与手部的几何关系，导致双臂交互时手腕相对位姿与指尖位置出现不匹配。

Method: 提出门控残差协调框架：保持两模块冻结，仅对其输出施加有界修正。包含运动条件动作门，用于在关节组间分配修正权限；以及依赖参考几何的奖励门，在训练中强调相关交互目标。为在 Agile One 上建立标称身体控制器，提出多姿态形态标定方法，联合估计三轴尺度与末端局部偏移，并采用分阶段动作数据集策展训练基于 SONIC 的跟踪器。残差策略仅使用人体运动参考、初始命令与机器人本体感知，不依赖显式的物体或接触观测。

Result: 在仿真中，方法在留出的 GRAB 动作上将手腕与指尖几何误差较直接组合降低 39.2-56.3%，同时在 AMASS 上保持全身跟踪。成功率为 89.03%（无残差协调）与 89.29%（含残差协调）。消融实验刻画了奖励门、自适应修正权限以及分离的身体与手部修正头的贡献。

Conclusion: 冻结现有模块并施加有界残差修正的思路，能够在无需显式物体或接触观测的情况下有效协调身体与手部几何关系，提升双臂交互的几何一致性，同时不损害全身跟踪性能。

Abstract: Whole-body humanoid teleoperation commonly combines a motion-tracking policy with a separate dexterous-hand retargeter. However, independently generated commands do not explicitly preserve body-hand geometric relations, leading to mismatches in relative wrist poses and fingertip positions during bimanual interaction. We present a gated residual coordination framework that keeps both modules frozen and applies bounded corrections to their outputs. A motion-conditioned action gate allocates correction authority across joint groups, while reference-geometry-dependent reward gates emphasize relevant interaction objectives during training. To establish the nominal body controller on Agile One, we introduce multi-pose morphology calibration that jointly estimates triaxial scales and effector-local offsets, together with staged motion dataset curation for training a SONIC-based tracker. The residual policy uses human motion references, initial commands, and robot proprioception without explicit object or contact observations. In simulation, it reduces wrist and fingertip geometry errors by 39.2-56.3% over direct composition on held-out GRAB motions, while preserving whole-body tracking on AMASS, with success rates of 89.03% without residual coordination and 89.29% with it. Ablations characterize the contributions of reward gating, adaptive correction authority, and separate body and hand correction heads.

</details>


### [364] [TRACER: Adaptive Multi-Robot Social Navigation via Joint Human-Response Prediction and Interaction-Aware Replanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18776)
*Lan Hu,Minghui Liwang,Wenbo Zhu,Xinlei Yi,Wei Gong,Yiguang Hong,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACER 是一个双向滚动时域框架，将动作条件预测、多机器人规划与在线自适应统一为闭环，通过分解单体效应与非加性成对交互的响应模型以及身份绑定的持久信念更新，提升多机器人社会导航的安全与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人导航方法通常将动作条件预测、多机器人规划或在线自适应分开处理，缺乏统一机制来建模机器人与周边实体的联合交互，并从已执行的交互结果中改进后续决策。论文旨在弥补这一空白，实现预测与自适应之间的闭环。

Method: 提出 TRACER 双向滚动时域框架：（1）对候选轨迹（机器人团队未来可行备选运动计划）进行评估；（2）使用逐实体概率响应模型，将单机器人效应与非加性成对交互分离建模；（3）执行所选轨迹前段后，利用同步观测到的响应更新身份绑定的潜在响应模式信念；（4）更新后的信念在概率安全与响应感知代价准则下指导后续候选评估。

Result: 实验表明：（i）TRACER 相比容量匹配的加性预测器能更准确捕捉非加性多机器人交互效应；（ii）持续的身份一致证据改善了响应预测与下游重规划；（iii）在 SocialGym2 多机器人社会导航基准上，完整 TRACER 框架相比独立机器人基线提升了无碰撞完成率。

Conclusion: TRACER 通过预测与自适应的双向闭环以及非加性逐实体交互建模，有效提升了多机器人社会导航中的响应预测与无碰撞完成表现。

Abstract: Multi-robot navigation in human-shared spaces is inherently interactive: coordinated robot motions influence how nearby entities respond, while those responses provide valuable information for subsequent robot decisions. However, existing methods typically address action-conditioned prediction, multi-robot planning, or online adaptation separately, and therefore lack a unified mechanism for modeling joint robot-entity interactions and adapting future decisions from executed interaction outcomes. To address this gap, we propose TRACER, a bi-directional receding-horizon framework that closes the loop between prediction and adaptation. TRACER evaluates candidate (i.e., alternative feasible future motion plans for the robot team) trajectories using a per-entity probabilistic response model that separates individual-robot effects from non-additive pairwise interactions; after executing the selected trajectory prefix, it updates persistent identity-bound beliefs over latent response modes using the synchronized observed responses. These updated beliefs then guide subsequent candidate evaluation under probabilistic safety and response-aware cost criteria. Experiments show that (i) TRACER more accurately captures non-additive multi-robot interaction effects than a capacity-matched additive predictor, (ii) persistent identity-consistent evidence improves response prediction and downstream replanning, and (iii) the complete TRACER framework improves collision-free completion over an independent-robot baseline on the SocialGym2 multi-robot social-navigation benchmark.

</details>


### [365] [AdaGeoVLN: Selective Geometry Across Representation Depth and Navigation Time for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18789)
*Quan-Dung Pham,Anh Dao,Danh Vinh Le,Nguyen Viet Tri Pham,The-Anh Nguyen,Zhirui Dai,Yiyu Chen,Tuyen P. Le,Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种流式视觉语言导航（VLN）框架，通过分层几何基础模型（GFM）与VLM融合以及导航感知的GFM记忆机制，在表示深度与导航时间两个维度上解决几何特征利用与历史证据保留问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN需要在时间维度上对齐语言与视觉观测并保持空间理解。几何基础模型（GFM）在层级中暴露中间表示，但导航策略应如何使用这些特征、如何保留历史几何证据仍未解决。

Method: 提出分层GFM–VLM融合，将GFM早期、中期、后期表示分别耦合到连续策略阶段，而非重复注入末端特征；并设计导航感知GFM记忆，依据指令相关性、几何置信度和转移新颖性，在有界的逐层预算下保留历史VGGT全局注意力KV状态，在融合前为后续观测提供几何上下文。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE上，仅使用单一RGB流且无需额外导航专用外部数据即取得强性能。消融实验表明，多深度耦合在相同融合位置下显著优于重复末端特征注入；有界导航感知保留在维持导航性能的同时，相比更大内存的时间保留大幅降低GFM-KV内存。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持同时审视暴露给策略的几何表示以及为未来推断而保留的历史证据，为流式VLN中几何特征利用与记忆管理提供有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation requires aligning language with visual observations while maintaining spatial understanding over time. Geometry foundation models (GFMs) expose intermediate representations throughout their hierarchy, but how navigation policies should use these features and retain historical geometric evidence remains unresolved. We introduce \method{}, a streaming VLN framework that addresses these questions across \textbf{representation depth} and \textbf{navigation time}. Hierarchical GFM--VLM fusion couples earlier, intermediate, and later GFM representations to successive policy stages instead of repeatedly injecting a terminal feature. Navigation-aware GFM memory retains historical VGGT global-attention KV states according to instruction relevance, geometric confidence, and transition novelty under a bounded per-layer budget. Retained states provide geometric context for subsequent observations before fusion with the policy. Across R2R-CE and RxR-CE, \method{} achieves strong performance using a single RGB stream without additional navigation-specific external data. Controlled ablations show that multi-depth coupling substantially outperforms repeated terminal-feature injection at matched fusion locations. Bounded navigation-aware retention preserves navigation performance while considerably reducing GFM-KV memory relative to larger-memory temporal retention. These findings support jointly examining the geometric representations exposed to the policy and the historical evidence retained for future inference. Code will be released upon acceptance at https://humanoid-research.github.io/adageovln/.

</details>


### [366] [Asymptotically Optimal Multi-Robot Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18813)
*Thi Thuy Ngan Duong,Cheuk Tung Shadow Yiu,Rahul Shome,Yoonchang Sung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究多机器人任务与运动规划（MR-TAMP）的渐近最优性，刻画了任务转移结构并给出全局渐近最优的充分条件，据此提出结合演化单机器人路线图与隐式张量积搜索的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MR-TAMP需同时推理离散任务决策与多机器人连续无碰撞运动。已有渐近最优TAMP算法难以直接扩展到多机器人场景：不同任务转移涉及不同机器人子集，因而对复合构型空间施加不同维度的约束。因此渐近最优规划器不仅需在各任务模式内优化运动，还需充分探索连接不同模式的各类转移。本文旨在刻画该转移结构并建立全局渐近最优性条件。

Method: 作者刻画了MR-TAMP中的转移结构，建立全局渐近最优的充分条件：需持续覆盖相关转移，并在连通可行区域内渐近改进运动规划。基于这些条件，提出一种高效渐近最优MR-TAMP算法，将演化的单机器人路线图与隐式张量积搜索相结合，避免显式构建复合路线图；并采用条件转移采样、惰性碰撞检测以及模式级与解级引导来提升有限时间规划效率，同时保持持续探索。

Result: 所提框架在提升有限时间规划效率的同时保持持续探索，为多机器人操作提供了渐近最优性保证。

Conclusion: 该框架在多机器人操作中实现了渐近最优性保证，并高效利用了单机器人运动规划的结构。

Abstract: Multi-robot task and motion planning (MR-TAMP) requires jointly reasoning about discrete task decisions and continuous collision-free motions of multiple interacting robots. Although asymptotically optimal algorithms have been developed for task and motion planning, extending these guarantees to the multi-robot setting introduces an important challenge: different task transitions may involve different subsets of robots and therefore impose constraints of different dimensions on the composite configuration space. Consequently, an asymptotically optimal planner must not only optimize motion within each task mode, but also ensure sufficient exploration of the different types of transitions connecting them. We characterize this transition structure and establish sufficient conditions for global asymptotic optimality in MR-TAMP, requiring persistent coverage of relevant transitions and asymptotically improving motion planning within connected feasible regions. Based on these conditions, we develop an efficient asymptotically optimal MR-TAMP algorithm that combines evolving individual-robot roadmaps with implicit tensor-product search, avoiding explicit construction of the composite roadmap. The planner further employs conditional transition sampling, lazy collision checking, and mode- and solution-level guidance to improve finite-time planning efficiency while retaining persistent exploration. The resulting framework provides asymptotic optimality guarantees for multi-robot manipulation while efficiently exploiting the structure of individual-robot motion planning.

</details>


### [367] [SEAM: Submap-Anchored Evidence for Lifelong LiDAR Mapping under Trajectory Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18819)
*Kyuwon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SEAM：一种基于LiDAR的终身建图框架，通过子图级锚点优化轨迹生成证据，支持动态物体移除与变化检测，精度更高、速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于LiDAR的终身建图依赖单一全局锚点，导致轨迹后续修改时需重算全部证据，且会话间回环边几何可靠性难以保证，动态物体与环境变化的区分也不够精确。

Method: 1) 基于子图级锚点优化轨迹生成证据，通过子图级重投影使证据在轨迹被新会话修改后仍可用，无需从头重算；2) 采用基于DOP的置信度度量抑制几何不可靠的跨会话回环边，防止对齐误差；3) 提出方向性体素级证据模型，考虑随射线方向变化的占据模式。

Result: 在真实建筑工地数据集和长期多会话数据集上的实验表明，SEAM相比现有方法取得了更高的精度和更快的处理速度。

Conclusion: SEAM通过子图级锚点、DOP置信度抑制与方向感知证据模型，有效提升了LiDAR终身建图的精度与效率。

Abstract: We propose SEAM, a LiDAR-based lifelong mapping framework. Instead of relying on a single anchor spanning the entire session, SEAM generates evidence based on a trajectory optimized with submap-level anchors, and performs dynamic object removal and change detection. Through submap-level reprojection, the generated evidence remains usable even if the trajectory is subsequently modified by a new session, eliminating the need to recompute the entire process from scratch. SEAM suppresses geometrically unreliable inter-session loop edges using a DOP-based confidence measure. Suppressing unreliable loop edges prevents alignment errors. SEAM also uses a directional voxel-wise evidence model. The model accounts for occupancy patterns that vary with ray direction. Direction-aware evidence separates dynamic objects from environmental changes more precisely. Experiments on a real construction-site dataset and a long-term multi-session dataset show that SEAM achieves higher accuracy and faster processing than existing methods.

</details>


### [368] [KINO: A Keyframe Interface for VLM Planning and Whole-Body Control in Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18869)
*Sitong Chen,Fatemeh Zargarbashi,Jin Cheng,Tianxu An,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层框架，以运动关键帧作为VLM规划与RL控制的中间表示，实现人形机器人全身协调的物体抓取、搬运与放置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在执行全身协调运动的同时，需要理解任务指令与场景语义，直接将高层语义规划与底层关节控制衔接存在困难。

Method: 采用分层框架：VLM根据语言指令、场景观测与执行反馈，从预定义库中选择任务相关关键帧（指定目标全身位姿及物体位姿）；关键帧重定向到当前场景以适配物体位姿与尺寸；关键帧条件化的全身策略生成关节级动作。低层策略训练引入基于显著性的关键帧采样策略。

Result: 在稀疏VLM关键帧条件下，端到端任务成功率从44%提升至92%；在仿真与Unitree G1人形机器人上成功完成单手与双手操作，并能泛化到训练参考数据之外的放置位置。

Conclusion: 以关键帧为中间表示的分层框架有效连接语义规划与全身控制，且显著性采样显著提升训练效率与任务成功率。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires robots to interpret task instructions and scene semantics while executing coordinated whole-body motions. We propose a hierarchical framework that uses motion keyframes as an intermediate representation between Vision-Language Model (VLM) planning and Reinforcement Learning (RL) control. Each keyframe specifies a target whole-body robot pose and, when applicable, an object pose. Given a language instruction, scene observations, and execution feedback, the VLM selects successive task-relevant keyframes from a predefined library. The selected keyframes are retargeted to the current scene to account for object poses and dimensions. A keyframe-conditioned whole-body policy then generates joint-level actions to reach these goals. We introduce a saliency-based keyframe sampling strategy for low-level policy training that improves end-to-end task success rate from 44% to 92% when using sparse VLM keyframes. We evaluate our framework on object pickup, transport, and placement tasks in simulation and on a Unitree G1 humanoid. The system successfully performs both one- and two-handed manipulation and generalises to placement locations beyond the training reference data.

</details>


### [369] [Body-Motion Control of a Simulated Aerial Swarm from a First-Person View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18881)
*Yang Chen,Darius Giannoli,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种上身动作接口，用躯干倾斜、手部位置和头部旋转控制无人机集群的五个连续指令维度，实验表明其提升FPV导航效率但增加体力负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视角（FPV）遥操作空中集群时，操作者需同时协调集群的整体平移、观察方向和编队间距，传统遥控器在多维度连续控制上可能效率较低，因此需要更直观的操控接口。

Method: 设计上身接口，将躯干倾斜、手部位置、头部旋转映射为五个连续指令维度，并对每位参与者校准中立姿态与运动范围；14名被试在受试者内设计中，分别用该接口和传统遥控器在三维障碍赛道中导航15智能体仿真集群。

Result: 相比传统遥控器，身体动作控制使完成时间减少19.4%、质心路径长度减少7.0%，路径更直接；指令变化量降低88.8%，并发指令变化更频繁。门中心误差、收集产出、碰撞或断连次数、整体工作负荷和可用性无显著差异；所有参与者报告身体动作控制的体力需求更高。

Conclusion: 该接口在FPV导航效率上有所提升，但代价是更高的体力需求。

Abstract: First-person-view (FPV) teleoperation of aerial swarms requires an operator to coordinate collective translation, viewing direction, and formation spacing. We present an upper-body interface that maps torso inclination, hand position, and head rotation to five continuous command dimensions. Neutral postures and motion ranges are calibrated for each participant. In a within-subject study, 14 participants navigated a simulated 15-agent swarm through three-dimensional obstacle courses using this interface and a conventional transmitter. Body-motion control reduced completion time by 19.4% and centroid path length by 7.0%, and increased path directness. Delivered-command variation was 88.8% lower, and concurrent command changes were more frequent. These command measures characterize the complete interfaces, which differed in calibration and filtering. No differences were detected in gate-centering error, collection yield, crash or disconnection counts, overall workload, or usability. All participants reported higher physical demand with body-motion control. The implemented interface therefore improved FPV navigation efficiency at the cost of greater physical demand.

</details>


### [370] [SOL-SLAM: Inverse Compositional Gauss-Newton Direct Registration for Fast Sonar-Only Local SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18893)
*Kalvik Jakkala,Jason O'Kane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖前视声呐（FLS）的稠密直接配准局部SLAM方法，将完整声呐强度扫描与递归更新的局部地图对齐，并用逆合成高斯-牛顿优化实现实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下导航通常依赖复杂昂贵的多模态传感器以实现全局SLAM精度，但粗导航与避障等局部反应行为只需局部一致性，理论上仅用FLS即可实现；然而该方向研究不足。此外，现有声学SLAM框架多依赖稀疏特征提取，丢弃了大量本就信息稀疏的声呐回波数据。

Method: 提出稠密直接配准方法，将完整声学强度扫描与递归更新的局部地图对齐；采用逆合成高斯-牛顿优化策略降低计算开销，实现实时执行。

Result: 实验表明该方法在平移误差上显著优于稀疏关键点基线，在较大位移间隔下仍保持稳定的亚米级跟踪精度；在特征丰富环境中，其里程计性能可与FLS、DVL、IMU多传感器融合方案相当，从而省去昂贵载荷依赖。通过AUV实地试验，在资源受限的嵌入式计算机上机载运行完整局部SLAM验证了实际可用性。

Conclusion: 仅用FLS的稠密直接方法可有效支撑局部SLAM，取得优于稀疏基线的精度并与多传感器融合相当，且能在嵌入式平台上实时运行，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater navigation typically relies on complex and expensive multi-modal sensor suites designed to prioritize global Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) accuracy. However, local reactive behaviors such as coarse navigation and obstacle avoidance require only local consistency---a capability that should be feasible using only a Forward-Looking Sonar (FLS), yet remains largely unaddressed, leaving a critical gap in FLS-only local SLAM.
  Moreover, existing acoustic SLAM frameworks predominantly rely on sparse feature extraction methods that discard substantial portions of the already information-sparse acoustic returns. To overcome these limitations, this work introduces a dense direct registration approach that aligns full acoustic intensity scans to a recursively updated local map. Real-time execution is achieved via an Inverse Compositional Gauss-Newton optimization strategy that minimizes computational overhead.
  Experimental evaluations show that this dense method yields significant improvements on translation error compared to sparse keypoint baselines, maintaining stable sub-meter tracking precision over wide displacement gaps. Moreover, this approach delivers odometry performance comparable to multi-sensor fusion pipelines (FLS, DVL, and IMU), bypassing expensive payload dependencies in feature-rich environments. We validate real-world applicability through AUV field trials, running the full local SLAM approach onboard an embedded, resource-constrained computer.

</details>


### [371] [Examining the Difference in Human Behavior Between Virtual and Real-World Human-Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18900)
*Sean Dallas,Absalat Getachew,Motaz AbuHijleh,Andrea Macklem-Zabel,Douglas Zytko,Mark Brudnak,Wing-Yue Geoffrey Louie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过用户研究比较了虚拟与现实环境中人机团队（HRT）场景下的人类行为差异，采用混合方法考察团队绩效与人因因素，发现两种模态在工作负荷、策略、机器人心理模型及信任类型上存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实世界中构建和评估人机团队（HRT）场景成本高昂，虚拟仿真被用作替代方案来研究用户感知、行为与绩效。然而，虚拟实验的有效性依赖于人类行为在真实与虚拟世界之间的一致性，因此需要检验两种模态下行为是否存在差异。

Method: 作者开展了一项用户研究，将同一HRT场景分别置于虚拟环境和真实环境中进行，采用混合研究方法（定量与定性结合）来考察团队绩效和人类因素。

Result: 定量结果显示，两种模态之间在工作负荷上存在显著差异。定性分析进一步拓展了定量结果，发现参与者在策略、对机器人的心理模型以及对机器人的信任类型方面，在两种模态间存在差异。

Conclusion: 虚拟与现实HRT场景下的人类行为并不完全一致，二者在工作负荷及多种主观与策略性因素上存在差异，这提示使用虚拟实验研究人机交互时需谨慎对待其外部效度。

Abstract: Prototyping and evaluating human-robot teaming (HRT) scenarios in the real-world is costly. Virtual simulation of HRT scenarios has been adopted as an alternative to conducting user studies in the real-world to investigate user perceptions, behaviors, and performance during human-robot interactions. The consistency of human behavior between the real and virtual-worlds is integral to the validity of utilizing such virtual experimentation. This paper presents a user study examining the difference in human behavior between an HRT scenario conducted in a virtual vs real environment. We employed mixed-methods to examine team performance and human factors during the HRT. Our quantitative results showed a significant difference in workload between the two modalities. Our qualitative analysis expanded on the quantitative results and found differences in participants' strategy, their mental model of robots, and the type of trust they had for robots between modalities.

</details>


### [372] [CaSCo: Cascade-Aware Soft-Collision Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18910)
*Shivaram Kumar,Gaoyuan Liu,Yoonchang Sung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出CaSCo框架，将碰撞视为具有不同语义风险的软约束，并结合视觉语言模型与物理模拟器预测级联碰撞后果，通过带可采纳启发式的最优图搜索规划低语义风险的运动路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动规划将碰撞简化为二元约束，忽略了与不同物体接触的后果差异（如纸箱可安全擦碰，而玻璃、笔记本电脑或不稳定物体即使轻微接触也不可取）。此外，机器人与物体的直接碰撞可能移动物体并触发次级物体间碰撞，使运动风险取决于场景的物理演化而非仅几何路径。

Method: 提出CaSCo级联感知软碰撞运动规划框架：由视觉语言模型或语言模型为物体分配语义风险，由物理模拟器预测候选机器人运动的后果；搜索使直接或间接（级联碰撞）位移的唯一物体总语义风险最小的路径。由于碰撞会改变环境，将路图状态扩展为包含预测的物体布局和已承担风险的物体集合，并开发带可采纳且一致的级联松弛启发式的最优图搜索算法，辅以缓存与剪枝机制提升搜索效率。

Result: 在杂乱操作环境中进行实验，评估语义风险、级联推理、规划效率以及真机运行表现。

Conclusion: CaSCo将碰撞风险从二元判断提升为语义化、考虑级联效应的软约束规划，实验涵盖风险、推理、效率与真机验证。

Abstract: Conventional motion planning treats collision as a binary constraint, although contact with different objects can have drastically different consequences. A robot may safely brush against a cardboard box while even minor contact with a glass, laptop, or unstable object may be undesirable. Moreover, a direct robot--object collision can move the contacted object and trigger secondary object--object collisions, making the risk of a motion depend on the physical evolution of the scene rather than only on the robot's geometric path. We present CaSCo, a cascade-aware soft-collision motion planning framework in which a vision-language or language model assigns semantic risk to objects and a physics simulator predicts the consequences of candidate robot motions. CaSCo searches for a path that minimizes the total semantic risk of the unique objects displaced either directly by the robot or indirectly through cascaded collisions. Because collisions change the environment, we augment roadmap states with the predicted object arrangement and the set of objects whose risk has already been incurred. We develop an optimal graph-search algorithm with an admissible and consistent cascade-relaxed heuristic and caching and pruning mechanisms for efficient search. Experiments in cluttered manipulation environments evaluate semantic risk, cascade reasoning, planning efficiency, and real-robot operation.

</details>


### [373] [Learning Holistic Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation with a Bipedal Mobile Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18930)
*Zhongyu Chen,Yuxuan Nai,Qian Chen,Yidong Zhu,Chen Jing,Qihan Wang,Xudong Li,Zhizhan Li,Leixin Chang,Liangjing Yang,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用强化学习训练的统一下肢-操作全身控制器，将6自由度末端执行器目标直接映射为双足底座与机械臂的协调动作，实现无显式底座速度或步态指令下的伸展、姿态调整与迈步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足机器人的移动-操作能力可突破手臂固有工作空间，但要实现这一能力，需要底层全身控制器将任务级操作目标转化为协调的手臂与腿部运动并保持平衡。现有方法通常依赖显式的底座速度或落脚点指令，缺乏统一的端到端接口。

Method: 采用强化学习训练统一的全身控制器，直接以6-DoF末端执行器目标为输入，输出双足底座与机械臂的协调动作。训练中使用奖励门控策略调节末端跟踪、行走与平衡之间的权衡；并使用时间上下文估计器，结合窗口化Transformer编码、循环GRU记忆与辅助动力学预测，从观测历史中提取动力学相关信息。

Result: 真机实验表明，同一控制器可在VR遥操作、学习得到的扩散策略以及脚本轨迹三种指令来源下，支持伸展、姿态调整与迈步，为多种操作任务提供统一的末端执行器接口。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了仅以末端执行器目标为输入的强化学习全身控制器可自主协调伸展、姿态调整与迈步并保持平衡，为双足移动-操作提供了一种通用的末端执行器接口。

Abstract: Bipedal loco-manipulation enables robots to interact with objects beyond the nominal workspace of their arms by coordinating locomotion and manipulation. Realizing this capability requires a low-level whole-body controller that translates task-level manipulation goals into coordinated arm and leg motions while maintaining balance. We present a unified whole-body controller trained with reinforcement learning that directly maps 6-DoF end-effector targets to coordinated actions for the bipedal base and robotic arm. Given only an end-effector target, the learned controller autonomously coordinates reaching, postural adaptation, and stepping without explicit base-velocity or footstep commands. A reward-gating strategy regulates the trade-offs among end-effector tracking, locomotion, and balance during training, while a temporal context estimator combines windowed Transformer encoding, recurrent GRU memory, and auxiliary dynamics prediction to extract dynamics-relevant information from observation history. Real-robot experiments demonstrate that the same controller supports reaching, postural adaptation, and stepping under commands from VR teleoperation, a learned diffusion policy, and scripted trajectories, providing a common end-effector interface for diverse manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [374] ["What's going to happen after I'm gone?": Parent Perspectives on Technology in Supporting Independent Living for Adults with Intellectual Disabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18970)
*Alexander Tyshka,Andrea Macklem-Zabel,Absalat Getachew,Foong Ling Chen,Wing-Yue Geoffrey Louie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过对16位智力与发展性障碍（IDD）成年子女家长的访谈与主题分析，提炼出四项面向成长的支持性技术设计原则，以助力IDD成年人从家庭向半独立生活的过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IDD成年人正日益从家庭转向半独立生活，家长在卸任主要照护者角色时面临持续、优质支持的安排难题。家长掌握子女需求的显性与隐性知识，理解其照护惯例是识别辅助技术如何有效发挥作用的基础。

Method: 访谈16位正处于过渡初期的IDD成年人家长，围绕三方面展开：1）当前日常支持方式及有效性；2）对技术使用的经验与看法；3）对辅助技术融入新居或与其他支持协同以促进独立的设想。采用主题分析法。

Result: 主题分析得到四个主题：常见支持模式；日常惯例中促进成长；跨过渡期照护连续性的脆弱性；参与者对辅助技术的认知与体验。据此推导出四项设计原则：过渡前入职以捕获照护者隐性知识；面向长期成长的结构化脚手架；响应日常波动的自适应感知；个体化定制与照护网络一致性之间的平衡。

Conclusion: 基于家长视角的照护惯例研究为辅助技术设计提供了成长导向的原则，有望在IDD成年人向半独立生活过渡中支持其独立性并缓解照护连续性脆弱问题。

Abstract: Adults with intellectual and developmental disabilities (IDD) are increasingly transitioning from family homes towards semi-inde\-pendent living. As parents hand off the role of primary caregiver, they face numerous challenges in arranging consistent and quality support. Our research centers on understanding these caregiving routines. By focusing on the unique lived experience of parents, who possess extensive explicit and tacit knowledge of their adult child's requirements, we aim to map the management of care they provide. This foundational understanding is essential to identifying how assistive technologies can effectively serve a key role in supporting adults with IDD in this transition. In this work, we interviewed 16 parents of adults with IDD beginning this transition to understand: 1) how they currently provide support for daily living and what makes their support effective; 2) what their experiences and perceptions are regarding the use of technology; and 3) how they envision assistive technology successfully integrating with their adult child's new home or with other supports to promote independence. Our thematic analysis produced four themes: common modes of support, inside the routine to support growth, the fragility of continuity of care across transitions, and participants' perceptions and experiences of assistive technology. Building on these findings, we derive four design principles for growth-oriented assistive technology. These include pre-transition onboarding to capture caregiver tacit knowledge; structured scaffolding towards long-term growth; adaptive sensing that responds to day-to-day variability; and customization for the individual balanced with consistency for the care network.

</details>


### [375] [Information-Based Trajectory Planning for Spacecraft-to-Spacecraft Tracking and Navigation in Cislunar Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19012)
*Trevor N. Wolf,Brandon A. Jones,Jay W. McMahon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轨迹规划方法，在信息收集与控制代价之间取得平衡，用于改进地月空间的航天器间绝对跟踪，并在DRO光学观测场景中实现近一个数量级的导航与跟踪误差改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地月空间使用日益扩展，需减少对地面支持的依赖，发展替代导航与跟踪手段；航天器间跟踪利用相对测量中的非线性动力学信息推断观测者与目标的绝对状态，而航天器间几何关系显著影响跟踪性能，因此需要设计能兼顾信息收益与控制代价的观测者轨迹。

Method: 将信息论量化的预期航天器间跟踪性能与控制代价联合纳入最优控制问题，设计观测者轨迹；方法面向多低推力观测平台与多种传感模式，并可在规划中纳入多个空间目标。

Result: 在最优控制问题中利用信息增益后，对运行于远距离逆行轨道（DRO）的光学观测者，预期导航与跟踪误差改善近一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了信息最优的低推力航天器轨迹设计在当前及未来地月任务中的可行性与价值。

Abstract: We present a trajectory planning method that balances information collection and control effort to improve cislunar spacecraft-to-spacecraft absolute tracking. Expanding use of cislunar space requires alternative navigation and tracking procedures that minimize reliance on ground-based support. Among efforts to address this need, spacecraft-to-spacecraft tracking exploits nonlinear dynamical tracers encoded in a series of relative measurements to infer absolute states of both an observer and a target. The geometry between spacecraft operating under this mode can significantly influence tracking performance. This work considers this geometrical impact by designing observer trajectories that jointly balance control effort and an information-theoretic quantification of the expected spacecraft-to-spacecraft tracking performance. Our methods are designed for multiple low-thrust observation platforms of various sensing modalities and can incorporate multiple space object targets in planning. By leveraging information gain in the optimal control problem, we report almost an order of magnitude improvement in the expected navigation and tracking errors for an optical observer operating in a Distant Retrograde Orbit (DRO). This work demonstrates the feasibility and value of information-optimal low-thrust spacecraft trajectory design for current and upcoming cislunar missions.

</details>


### [376] [Learning to Stack: Cube-Stacking Imitation Learning from Virtual Reality Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19040)
*Gryffin Reizian,Jordan Dowdy,Jean Chagas Vaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于VR的虚拟演示采集流程，在Isaac Sim/Lab中用自定义5自由度机械臂完成立方体堆叠任务，实现采集与数据集构建解耦，30分钟内采集200条虚拟演示并训练行为克隆策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习中，演示数据采集是瓶颈，尤其对于需要反复重置场景的多阶段任务；真实世界采集成本高、效率低，且已采集的演示难以适配新的观测与动作空间。

Method: 构建VR数据采集流程：使用HTC Vive Pro 2、Manus Quantum手套与OpenXR，在NVIDIA Isaac Sim和Isaac Lab中为自定义5自由度机械臂提供SE(3)末端执行器指令以生成演示；框架将演示采集与数据集构建分离，通过重放轨迹、将任务空间指令转换为关节空间动作，并重新渲染更新后的传感器或状态配置，从而复用已有演示。任务为随机摆放下的红蓝绿三立方体依次堆叠，另用Isaac Mimic生成100条额外样本，并以LeRobot风格双相机观测训练行为克隆策略。

Result: 30分钟内采集200条虚拟演示，对比45条真实世界演示，并用Isaac Mimic额外生成100条样本；基于虚拟演示训练的行为克隆策略在仿真中完成评估。

Conclusion: 该VR流程显著提升了多阶段操作任务的演示采集效率，并通过采集与数据构建解耦实现演示复用，为虚拟演示训练机器人操作策略提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Imitation learning is attractive for robot manipulation, but collecting demonstrations remains a bottleneck for multi-stage tasks requiring repeated scene resets. This work presents a virtual-reality data-collection pipeline for cube-stacking with a custom 5-DoF arm in NVIDIA Isaac Sim and Isaac Lab. Using an HTC Vive Pro 2, Manus Quantum gloves, and OpenXR, an operator provides SE(3) end-effector commands to generate task demonstrations. The proposed framework separates demonstration collection from dataset construction by replaying recorded trajectories, converting task-space commands into joint-space actions, and re-rendering demonstrations with updated sensor or state configurations. This allows previously collected demonstrations to be reused for new observation and action spaces without repeating human teleoperation. The task requires stacking the red cube on the blue cube and the green cube on the red cube, with randomized cube placement. In 30 minutes, 200 virtual demonstrations were collected, compared with 45 real-world demonstrations, and Isaac Mimic generated 100 additional samples. A behavior-cloning policy was trained from the virtual demonstrations using LeRobot-style dual-camera observations and evaluated in simulation.

</details>


### [377] [Loco-Loco-RL: Low-Cost Terrain Mapping for Humanoid Locomotion with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19041)
*Jordan Dowdy,Gryffin Reizian,Jean Chagas Vaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用低成本飞行时间传感器获取紧凑三维局部地形表示，并设计一种token压缩时序Transformer策略，实现人形机器人稳健地形行走的仿真到真实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人强化学习策略需要信息丰富的地形感知，但深度相机和LiDAR等常见传感器成本高、功耗大、处理开销高，且常产生冗余的高分辨率数据。因此需要一种低成本、紧凑的局部地形感知方案来支撑稳健的行走策略。

Method: 采用低成本飞行时间（ToF）传感器提供紧凑的3D局部地形表示；提出token压缩时序Transformer策略：将本体感知与地形观测token化，经自注意力多头Transformer捕获同一时间步内各观测项之间的关系；通过基于MLP的隐空间token压缩模块压缩注意力token，再存入15步滚动历史；第二层交叉注意力多头Transformer从紧凑历史中提取时序运动特征用于策略学习。压缩在时序聚合之前进行，从而保留关键地形观测结构并限制注意力随历史维度增长。

Result: 在基于地形的运动基准上，通过仿真到真实（sim-to-real）迁移在物理硬件上进行了验证，实现了使用低成本局部地形感知的稳健人形机器人地形行走。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，低成本ToF传感器结合token压缩时序Transformer可有效支撑人形机器人在真实地形上的稳健行走，兼顾了感知效率与策略性能。

Abstract: Informative terrain perception is important for robust reinforcement learning policies in humanoid locomotion. Still, common sensors such as depth cameras and LiDARs incur high cost, power, and processing overhead while often producing redundant, high-resolution data. This work uses a low-cost time-of-flight sensor to provide a compact 3D local terrain representation for humanoid locomotion. To efficiently use this sparse exteroceptive input, we introduce a token-compressed temporal transformer policy. Proprioceptive and terrain observations are tokenized and processed by a self-attention multi-head transformer to capture within-timestep relationships between observation terms. The attended tokens are then compressed through an MLP-based latent-space token compression module before being stored in a rolling 15-timestep history. A second cross-attention multi-head transformer extracts temporal locomotion features from this compact history for policy learning. By compressing tokens before temporal aggregation, the architecture preserves important terrain-observation structure while limiting the dimensional growth of attention over observation histories. We validate our method through sim-to-real transfer on physical hardware using a terrain-based locomotion benchmark, demonstrating robust humanoid terrain walking with low-cost local terrain sensing.

</details>


### [378] [ElastiQP: An Always-Feasible QP Solver for Constrained Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19080)
*Daniel Morton,Jon Arrizabalaga,Zachary Manchester,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ElastiQP：一种改进的对偶主动集QP求解器，通过对不等式约束施加精确的逐约束l1惩罚、将松弛变量解析折叠进求解器，保持线性系统规模恒定，在可行问题上达到微秒级性能，在不可行问题上优雅返回可用解且比最佳替代求解器快达40倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人能力增强导致QP控制器需处理越来越多的约束，每增加一个约束就增加瞬时冲突的可能。当QP求解器返回"不可行"状态时，控制器无解可执行，因此需要一种能在冲突时仍返回可用解的求解器。

Method: 提出ElastiQP，一种修改的对偶主动集QP求解器：对每个不等式约束施加精确的逐约束l1惩罚进行松弛，同时保持等式约束（动力学）为硬约束；关键是将松弛变量解析地折叠进求解器，从而保持凝聚线性系统的规模恒定。

Result: 在机器人控制基准测试套件上，ElastiQP达到微秒级性能，在可行问题上匹配或优于领先的现代求解器；在不可行问题上，将违反限制严格局限于冲突的不等式项，返回可用解的速度比最佳替代求解器快达40倍。

Conclusion: ElastiQP能有效处理QP控制器的约束冲突问题，兼顾可行问题的高性能与不可行问题的优雅降级。该求解器已作为开源C++ header-only库发布，并提供Python和JAX接口，地址为https://github.com/StanfordASL/elastiqp。

Abstract: As robot capabilities increase, quadratic programming (QP)-based controllers must account for a similarly increasing number of constraints to ensure safe, reliable operation. Yet, with each added constraint, this introduces more chances of momentary conflict: in which case, a QP solver that returns an "infeasible" status leaves the controller with nothing to execute. To address this, we introduce ElastiQP, a modified dual active-set QP solver that relaxes every inequality constraint with an exact, per-constraint l1 penalty while keeping equality constraints (dynamics) hard. Notably, ElastiQP does so by folding the slack variables into the solver analytically, maintaining a constant size of the condensed linear system. On a suite of robot control benchmarks, ElastiQP achieves microsecond-level performance, matching or outperforming leading modern solvers on feasible problems. On infeasible problems, ElastiQP handles these gracefully, confining violations to strictly the conflicting inequality terms, returning a usable solution up to 40x faster than the best alternative solvers. ElastiQP is available as an open-source C++ header-only library, with Python and JAX interfaces, at https://github.com/StanfordASL/elastiqp.

</details>


### [379] [rMuscle: Robotic Muscle Memory for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19104)
*Kaijun Zhou,Zhiyang Li,Le Chen,Jinyu Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: rMuscle是一个受人类肌肉记忆启发的实时VLA推理框架，通过双阶段肌肉记忆缓存复用跨执行的任务相似性，在RTX 4090和Jetson Thor上实现1.29-1.42倍加速且保持成功率不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂作业是具身AI的重要早期场景，VLA模型是当前主流策略范式，但其推理延迟直接影响机器人响应性与运动平滑度。现有VLA推理框架未充分利用具身工作负载特性，也未考虑VLA推理不同阶段的瓶颈差异。

Method: 首先刻画具身工作负载，发现重复机器人执行存在显著任务相似性，且该相似性不仅体现在观测和动作轨迹上，还延伸到内部模型状态。据此提出rMuscle框架，采用双阶段肌肉记忆缓存：上下文缓存复用视觉token输出以减少计算，动作缓存复用神经元激活模式以减少权重访问；并通过在线缓存重计算、滑窗缓存检索及连续去噪步间的掩码共享，降低缓存内存占用与访问开销。

Result: 在RTX 4090和Jetson Thor上，于LIBERO、RoboTwin及物理操作任务中实现1.29-1.42倍加速，同时在真实机器人上保持原有成功率。

Conclusion: 利用跨执行相似性及VLA推理的内部状态复用，可有效降低推理延迟，兼顾实时性与任务成功率，为具身AI工厂场景提供实用推理加速方案。

Abstract: Factory work is a promising early scenario for embodied AI: assigning repetitive manual jobs to robots has clear economic payoff, and a structured station keeps the jobs tractable for current policies. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models now dominate as the policy paradigm for these robots. The inference latency of VLA models directly affects robot responsiveness and motion smoothness. However, existing VLA inference frameworks do not fully exploit the characteristics of embodied workloads or account for the distinct bottlenecks across different stages of VLA inference.
  In this paper, we first characterize embodied workloads and identify substantial task similarity across repeated robot executions. We further find that such similarity extends beyond observations and action trajectories to internal model states. Drawing on these observations, we present rMuscle, a real-time VLA inference framework inspired by human muscle memory. It exploits cross-execution similarity through a dual-phase muscle-memory cache. The Context Cache reuses visual-token outputs to reduce computation, while the Action Cache reuses neuron activation patterns to reduce weight accesses. We keep both the cache memory footprint and access overhead low through online cache recomputation, sliding-window cache retrieval, and mask sharing across consecutive denoising steps. rMuscle achieves 1.29-1.42X speedup on RTX 4090 and Jetson Thor across LIBERO, RoboTwin, and physical manipulation tasks, while maintaining the original success rates on real-world robots.

</details>


### [380] [Dreaming the Sound of Contact: Leveraging Video and Audio Generation for Zero-Shot Force-Aware Manipulation and Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19137)
*Guanhua Ji,Tianyu Li,Dayoon Suh,Yuqian Zhang,Boyan Zhang,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用生成视频的音频响度塑造有界时变期望力曲线，让机器人完成接触丰富任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从生成视频学习操作轨迹的方法只产生纯运动学轨迹，缺少力信息，在需要合适接触力的接触丰富任务中会失败。

Method: 提出联合利用生成视频与音频的流程：从结构化自然语言任务提示中同时导出运动轨迹与期望力曲线，其中期望力曲线由生成接触声音的响度塑造（有界、时变）；在Franka Panda机器人上用闭环力调节器跟踪该音频塑形的力曲线。

Result: 在多个需要接触的任务上与纯运动学基线对比，成功完成操作而基线失败；并将该流程作为数据生成引擎，训练出可闭环完成任务的策略。

Conclusion: 引入生成音频来塑造接触力，可弥补生成视频轨迹缺乏力信息的缺陷，实现接触丰富任务的成功操作，并可用于策略训练的数据生成。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation allow robots to learn manipulation trajectories from generated videos. However, these approaches produce purely kinematic trajectories that lack force information, causing failures in contact-rich tasks where appropriate contact forces are essential for success. In this work, we explore augmenting generated video with audio to shape a bounded, time-varying desired-force profile using the loudness of generated contact sounds. We present a pipeline that jointly leverages generated video and audio to derive motion trajectories and corresponding desired-force profiles from a structured natural-language task prompt. We execute these force-aware trajectories on a Franka Panda robot using a closed-loop force regulator that tracks the audio-shaped force profile during contact. We evaluate our pipeline on multiple tasks that require making contact and demonstrate successful manipulation where a kinematic-only baseline fails. We also use the pipeline as a data generation engine to train policies that achieve the tasks in a closed-loop manner. Project website, videos, and dataset: https://dreamingcontactsound.github.io/

</details>
